# Task 4.2 Review-Correction Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Complete Task 4.2's historical proof with a history-preserving,
gate-only correction whose behavioral REDs, 25 implementer mutations, 25
independent controller replays, reviews, ignored evidence, frozen ledgers,
tracked evidence seal, and post-seal verifier are all mutually bound.

**Architecture:** Production commit
`622abf4d22232ecdf3dfa831d02a985578a145f3` remains byte-identical. Round
`r04` begins at a two-plan-path anchor commit whose exact parent is rejected
round `r03` tip P3
`98020fe367ab8dd922e2a2703502c0d8edb0db6a`; it creates no new test commit
and ends with a one-file evidence-seal commit whose parent is P4. A single
Apple-Bash-3.2 workflow driver is embedded literally in this tracked plan and
is always extracted from the exact P4 Git blob; it is never copied to or
sourced from another mutable helper. The existing forced-suite helper stays
ignored but is authenticated immediately before every invocation. Two ignored
append-only ledgers carry byte-identical, ordered 112-row `r04` suffixes; the
tracked seal freezes those ledgers and all 134 accepted-round artifact paths.
Rejected rounds `r01`, `r02`, and `r03` remain immutable prerequisites and never
satisfy an `r04` gate.

**Tech Stack:** C# 7.3; .NET SDK 10.0.300; compile-only CLR-v2/net35 target;
the repository's deterministic C# unit harness; offline `uv`/pytest; Git;
Apple `/bin/bash` 3.2.57 and standard macOS Tahoe 26.6 utilities.

## Global Constraints

- Work only in
  `/Users/jlsor/Documents/Research/SSR/.worktrees/ssr-oracle-passive-trace` on
  branch `codex/oracle-passive-driver-initial`.
- The round parent is exactly rejected `r03` anchor and tip P3
  `98020fe367ab8dd922e2a2703502c0d8edb0db6a`. P3's parent is corrected-test
  commit T2 `489cc1ce2a06e5a6db60759f5484ed2f170b4d5a`; T2's parent is P2
  `2471b92048592f13928969c04395f3e9495a476c`; P2's parent is rejected `r01`
  anchor `360436f8667407f5360fdf54701ab999a2729552`; the fixed ancestry then
  continues through correction design
  `b2eb61d0ab046fe613941fd60d9e28496db8511a` to immutable implementation
  `622abf4d22232ecdf3dfa831d02a985578a145f3`.
- Preserve all history. Never amend, rebase, squash, reset, delete, overwrite,
  or truncate any historical commit, patch, evidence file, or ledger byte.
- Before the first `r04` ledger append, the round anchor `P` must be a commit
  with parent `98020fe367ab8dd922e2a2703502c0d8edb0db6a`, subject
  `docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04`, and exactly the two plan paths listed
  below. The plan deliberately cannot contain `P`'s future object ID or its own
  SHA-256; Git supplies both after the commit.
- Round `r04` is gate-only: no new test commit exists. The corrected tests are
  inherited from exact T2, remain Git-clean, and are authenticated before and
  after every gate. P4 is both the accepted round tip and the final pre-seal
  correction commit.
- The evidence-seal commit `S` must have parent `P`, subject
  `docs: seal Task 4.2 correction evidence`, and exactly the one seal path.
- Do not modify `oracle/plugin/Core/PassiveDriver.cs`,
  `oracle/plugin/Core/PassiveDriverBoundaries.cs`, or
  `oracle/plugin/tests/Program.cs` except for authenticated, temporary mutation
  application followed by the mandatory inverse. They must always return to
  the literal hashes below.
- Do not change Task 5 behavior. The Task 5.1--5.3 ownership semantics remain
  the requirement, but none of the legacy Task 5.1--5.3 execution text is
  authorized after this correction. Even after S4 exists, Task 5 stays blocked
  until a descendant Task 5 ownership/rebase plan is written and approved from
  the sealed corrected hashes.
- Every `dotnet` command is offline and includes `--no-restore`. Every Python
  command uses `UV_OFFLINE=1`. Do not launch the game or a GUI, access a save,
  deploy, restore packages, use the network, or modify installed configuration.
- Never accept incremental build output after a failed compile. Net10 and
  net35 gates use `-warnaserror -t:Rebuild -m:1
  -p:UseSharedCompilation=false`. Focused and complete C# stdout must be exactly
  `SSR oracle unit harness ready`. Python must report exactly `1822 passed, 120
  xfailed, 6 xpassed`.
- `Git-clean` means an empty index, no tracked worktree diff, and no
  non-ignored untracked path. Preserved ignored build products and evidence do
  not violate Git-clean; they are validated separately.
- A partial output/status pair or a one-ledger-only append rejects `r04`.
  Never repair or reuse it. Preserve the partial bytes and start `r05` with a
  new tracked plan anchor, brief, namespace, prefix pins, and evidence seal.
- Preseal is captured and authenticated in a private mode-700 directory before
  either canonical path exists. Canonical output and status are then published
  as two separate noclobber writes. Never use `mv` or ordinary `cp` for that
  publication and never remove, truncate, complete, or retry a partial
  canonical pair.
- Private evidence creation uses `umask 077`; every authenticated helper
  invocation instead runs in a narrow child with explicit `umask 022`. The
  helper, tests, production sources, and accepted transcript grammar stay
  byte-identical. Once complete, a private preseal bundle contains exactly
  `diagnostic.txt`, `output.txt`, and `status.txt`, each mode 600. A failure
  before that completion preserves an authenticated setup residual; a failure
  after completion but before cleanup commit preserves the authenticated exact
  bundle; and a failure after cleanup commit preserves an authenticated cleanup
  residual. Exact cleanup occurs once, only after public proof and ledger
  closure, and never reconstructs a removed private file.
- Serialize every operation that can change tracked files, the index, HEAD,
  ignored artifacts, or either ledger. Only the four Task 6 read-only reviews
  may overlap. While they run, no other worker may invoke the driver or touch
  repository/evidence state; the controller appends their accepted rows later,
  one at a time in canonical order.
- Before executing any fenced shell block, extract it as directed and require
  `/bin/bash -n` to pass. Do not translate the embedded driver to zsh or a
  Bash-4/GNU dialect. The embedded driver disables brace expansion immediately
  after enabling strict mode; this is part of its authenticated Apple Bash 3.2
  contract and prevents quoted C# brace literals from becoming shell words.

## Rejected `r01` record

- Anchor and tracked tip:
  `360436f8667407f5360fdf54701ab999a2729552`; parent
  `b2eb61d0ab046fe613941fd60d9e28496db8511a`; subject
  `docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r01`; exact two-plan-path scope.
- Task 1 completed and appended exactly 44 byte-identical rows to the two
  ledgers. The `r01` manifest remained exactly 26 materialized paths and 108
  reserved absent paths.
- Task 2 applied the authenticated two-test patch, then the authoritative
  baseline launcher exited `2` before RED, build, helper, mutation, or evidence
  execution with `/bin/bash: line 201: test: too many arguments`.
- Root cause: Apple Bash 3.2 brace-expanded the quoted C# manifest literal in
  the embedded driver into extra `awk` arguments. An in-memory driver with only
  `set +o braceexpand` added after strict mode passed the complete baseline.
- The exact inverse restored both tests to their pre-correction hashes. No
  `r01` test commit or evidence seal exists. All `r01` commits, ledger bytes,
  and ignored artifacts remain immutable and cannot satisfy `r04`.

  `1878cb55370493ba6ba4af92e7d863117e60df13235ce23407cd0e97b5226a0c`.
  The rejected namespace is frozen at 132 materialized paths and the two

  harnesses, and the Python suite began. It published neither canonical
  preseal path. Its unconditional EXIT cleanup removed the private output and
  status, so no private r03 preseal file remains. No r03 seal exists.
- Root cause: `capture_preseal` set `umask 077` around `run_full_suite`, and the
  helper, `uv`, pytest, and fixtures inherited it. The active fixture was born
  mode `0600`; the `descriptor_mode` test then called `chmod(0600)`, made no
  mode change, and correctly elicited no production `InstallError`. The
  helper returned nonzero and the generic `|| return 1` chain erased its stage.
  Failure-time ignored pytest cache identified only that parametrization; it
  is diagnostic corroboration, not accepted correction evidence.
- All P3 commits, ledger bytes, 132 materialized ignored artifacts, the absent
  canonical pair, and absence of an r03 seal are immutable prerequisites and
  cannot satisfy any r04 gate. No r03 launcher or artifact may be retried,
  repaired, completed, or reused.

| Terminal/Task 5 handoff | root controller, read-only | exact `S4 -> P4 -> P3 -> T2` chain | live verifier result and descendant Task 5 rebase requirement |

## Accepted-round artifact manifest

The manifest has exactly 134 paths in this canonical order:

1. the correction brief;
2. `task-4.2-correction-r04-M01.patch` through `M25.patch`, numeric order;
3. RED output/status pairs for M03, M07, and M10, in that order;
4. implementer output/status pairs for M01 through M25;
5. controller output/status pairs for M01 through M25; and
6. the pre-seal output/status pair.

The shared helper and two ledgers are outside the 134 and are authenticated
separately. Phase counts are exact:

| Phase | Materialized | Reserved and absent |
|---|---:|---:|
| Plan anchor | 26 | 108 |
| After three REDs | 32 | 102 |
| After implementer M01--M25 | 82 | 52 |
| After controller M01--M25, before pre-seal | 132 | 2 |
| Closure and terminal verification | 134 | 0 |

For a reserved path, require both `! -e` and `! -L`. For a materialized path,
require a regular non-symlink and its exact recomputed contract. Reject every
extra `task-4.2-correction-r04-*` or correction-brief path in the accepted
round namespace. Prior-round and historical artifacts outside this manifest
remain preserved but cannot satisfy any `r04` gate.

The rejected namespaces are separate frozen 134-path manifests. `r01` has
exactly its brief and 25 authenticated patch files materialized (`26/108`).
`r02` has its brief, 25 patches, three RED pairs, and 25 implementer pairs
materialized (`82/52`); no review, controller, or preseal artifact exists.
`r03` has its brief, 25 patches, three RED pairs, 25 implementer pairs, and 25
controller pairs materialized (`132/2`); both preseal paths are absent.
Every driver mode verifies all three complete prior states independently before
inspecting or changing the `r04` namespace.

## Canonical 112-row ledger suffix

Every row begins exactly `Task 4.2 correction r04 ` and ends in one LF. The
same 112 rows, byte-for-byte and in this order, are appended to both ledgers:

1. `original execution base`
2. `original plan commit`
3. `immutable implementation commit`
4. `correction design commit`
5. `plan anchor commit`
6. `historical report prefix`
7. `historical progress prefix`
8. `anchor report prefix`
9. `anchor progress prefix`
10. `design artifact`
11. `supersession plan artifact`
12. `correction plan artifact`
13. `correction brief artifact`
14. `shared helper artifact`
15. `PassiveDriver artifact`
16. `PassiveDriverBoundaries artifact`
17. `Program artifact`
18. `PassiveDriverInitialTests artifact`
19. `PassiveDriverTestSupport artifact`
20--44. `patch M01 artifact` through `patch M25 artifact`
45--47. `red M03 evidence`, `red M07 evidence`, `red M10 evidence`
48. `post-RED full GREEN`
49--73. `implementer M01 evidence` through `implementer M25 evidence`
74. `clean P4 full suite one`
75. `clean P4 full suite two`
76. `test topology`
77. `round tip`
78. `final preseal correction commit`
79. `descendant chain`
80. `clean P4 full suite three`
81. `review specification`
82. `review behavioral mutation`
83. `review shell gate`
84. `review compatibility`
85--109. `controller M01 evidence` through `controller M25 evidence`
110. `controller replay summary`
111. `preseal evidence`
112. `preseal full suite`

Commit rows bind exact commit, parent, subject, and sorted path scope. Prefix
rows bind path, byte count, and SHA-256. Artifact rows bind path and SHA-256;
the two test artifact rows bind both before and corrected hashes plus corrected
line/byte counts. Each failure-evidence value has exactly this field order:

```text
output-path={canonical-output-path}|output-sha256={64-lowercase-hex}|status-path={canonical-status-path}|status-sha256={64-lowercase-hex}|marker={exact-role-marker}|registration={exact-registration}|fragment={exact-first-failure-fragment}|status=1|result=KILLED|restoration=GIT_CLEAN|focused-green=PASS
```

Review values have exactly
`identity={canonical-worker-identity}|tip={exact-P4}|verdict=APPROVE|critical=0|important=0`. The four
identities are distinct, contain no `|`, and correspond to fresh read-only
workers. Before every append, validate the complete anchored prefix and current
suffix. After every append, reject a missing, duplicate, unexpected,
out-of-order, malformed, non-LF-terminated, or unequal suffix. A partial append
rejects the round; it is never repaired.

## Corrected mutation matrix

Every patch is a regular non-symlink under
`.superpowers/sdd/2026-07-31-oracle-passive-plugin/` and is authenticated by
the literal SHA-256 below immediately before forward apply and again after
capture before reverse-check and inverse.

| ID | SHA-256 | Registration | Required first-failure fragment |

## Inherited corrected-test contract

No `r04` source or test edit is authorized. Exact parent T2 already contains
the supported-flow correction in exactly
`oracle/plugin/tests/PassiveDriverInitialTests.cs` and
`oracle/plugin/tests/PassiveDriverTestSupport.cs`. Every pre-seal mode requires
both corrected hashes and line/byte counts, an empty index, no tracked
worktree diff, no non-ignored untracked path, and `git diff --check` at exact
P4. Retain `SetCurrentFramesForDefensiveTest` and `RequirePrivateField` for
M14/M15, retain `AuthorizationProbe` and `ReadUpdateStage` for M24, and preserve
the eight registration names, their order, and the `driver-initial=8`
manifest. Any test/source drift rejects `r04`; it is never staged or committed
inside this round.

The correction is shell-only. It does not weaken or reinterpret the accepted
transcript grammar: each full-suite transcript still has exactly two successful
rebuild summaries, exactly two `SSR oracle unit harness ready` lines, exactly
one Python summary `1822 passed, 120 xfailed, 6 xpassed`, zero cohort failure
lines, and no forbidden diagnostic. The unchanged compatibility contract in
`tests/test_oracle_install_compat.py` is pinned above and reauthenticated by
every pre-seal mode; neither that file nor the forced-suite helper may be
edited to accommodate this correction.

## Commit-pinned embedded workflow driver

The next fenced block is executable source, not illustrative pseudocode. It is
the only controller-gate implementation for this round. Never save, edit, or
source a copy. Every invocation:

1. chooses the exact plan-anchor commit `P` from the documented topology;
2. proves that `P` has the required parent, subject, and two-path scope;
3. requires exactly one begin marker and one end marker in `P`'s plan blob;
4. extracts only the bytes between those markers from `P` with `git show` and
   `awk`;
5. passes those bytes through `/bin/bash -n`; and
6. extracts them again and executes `/bin/bash -s -- MODE P [arguments]`.

The driver itself must never use its working-tree copy as executable input.

```bash
# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_BEGIN
task42_main() {
set -euo pipefail
set +o braceexpand
local actual_branch
local actual_repo_root
local mode_status
local verified_plan_anchor

export PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin'
export LC_ALL=C
export GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1
export GIT_PAGER=cat
export DOTNET_CLI_UI_LANGUAGE=en
export DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1
export DOTNET_CLI_WORKLOAD_UPDATE_NOTIFY_DISABLE=true
export DOTNET_SDK_VULNERABILITY_CHECK_DISABLE=true
export DOTNET_NOLOGO=1
export DOTNET_SKIP_FIRST_TIME_EXPERIENCE=1

test "$#" -ge 2 || return 1
mode="$1"
plan_anchor="$2"
shift 2

case "$mode" in
    anchor|baseline|red|implementer|gate-suites|record-review|controller|preseal|emit-seal|verify-seal|pre-seal-commit|terminal) ;;
    *) exit 64 ;;
esac

test -z "${GIT_DIR-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_WORK_TREE-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_INDEX_FILE-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_ALTERNATE_OBJECT_DIRECTORIES-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_COMMON_DIR-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_NAMESPACE-}" || return 1
test -z "${GIT_REPLACE_REF_BASE-}" || return 1
test -z "${TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_HARNESS-}" || return 1
test -z "${TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_POINT-}" || return 1
test -z "${TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_PRIVATE_PREFIX-}" || return 1
test "${#plan_anchor}" = 40 || return 1
case "$plan_anchor" in
    *[!0-9a-f]*) exit 64 ;;
esac
verified_plan_anchor="$(git rev-parse \
    --verify "$plan_anchor^{commit}")" || return 1
test "$verified_plan_anchor" = "$plan_anchor" || return 1

repo_root='/Users/jlsor/Documents/Research/SSR/.worktrees/ssr-oracle-passive-trace'
branch='codex/oracle-passive-driver-initial'
round='r04'

sha256_file() {
    shasum -a 256 "$1" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }'
}

sha256_blob() {
    git show "$1:$2" | shasum -a 256 | awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }'
}

sha256_driver_blob() {
    driver_commit="$1"
    driver_begin="$2"
    driver_end="$3"
    git show "$driver_commit:$plan_path" |
        awk \
            -v begin="$driver_begin" \
            -v end="$driver_end" '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
    bc++
    if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
    inside=1
    next
}
$0 == end {
    ec++
    if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
    inside=0
    next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
    if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
    for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}' |
        shasum -a 256 | awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }'
}

emit_preseal_core_blob() {
    core_commit="$1"
    git show "$core_commit:$plan_path" |
        awk \
            -v begin='# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_BEGIN' \
            -v end='# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_END' '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
    bc++
    if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
    inside=1
    next
}
$0 == end {
    ec++
    if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
    inside=0
    next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
    if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
    for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}

preseal_core_blob_lines() {
    emit_preseal_core_blob "$1" | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

preseal_core_blob_bytes() {
    emit_preseal_core_blob "$1" | wc -c | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

preseal_core_blob_sha() {
    emit_preseal_core_blob "$1" |
        shasum -a 256 | awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }'
}

file_bytes() {
    wc -c < "$1" | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

file_lines() {
    wc -l < "$1" | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

require_exact_output() {
    local expected_output="$1"
    local actual_output
    shift
    actual_output="$("$@")" || return 1
    test "$actual_output" = "$expected_output" || return 1
}

require_regular_sha() {
    test -f "$1" || return 1
    test ! -L "$1" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$2" sha256_file "$1" || return 1
}

require_absent() {
    test ! -e "$1" || return 1
    test ! -L "$1" || return 1
}

require_empty_index() {
    require_exact_output '' git diff --cached --name-only || return 1
}

require_no_nonignored_untracked() {
    require_exact_output '' git ls-files --others --exclude-standard || return 1
}

require_git_clean() {
    require_empty_index || return 1
    require_exact_output '' git diff --name-only || return 1
    require_no_nonignored_untracked || return 1
    require_exact_output '' git status --short || return 1
    git diff --check || return 1
}

require_exact_commit() {
    local actual_scope
    commit="$1"
    parent="$2"
    subject="$3"
    expected_scope="$4"
    require_exact_output commit git cat-file -t "$commit" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$parent" git show -s --format='%P' "$commit" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$subject" git show -s --format='%s' "$commit" || return 1
    actual_scope="$(git diff-tree --no-commit-id --name-only -r "$commit" |
        LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1
    test "$actual_scope" = "$expected_scope" || return 1
}

plan_scope="$(printf '%s\n' \
    "$legacy_plan_path" "$plan_path" | LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1
test_scope="$(printf '%s\n' \
    "$initial_tests_path" "$support_path" | LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1
implementation_scope="$(printf '%s\n' \
    "$driver_path" "$boundaries_path" "$initial_tests_path" \
    "$support_path" "$program_path" | LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1

require_plan_anchor() {
    require_exact_commit "$plan_anchor" "$round_parent" \
        'docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04' "$plan_scope" || return 1
}

require_fixed_history() {
    require_exact_commit "$original_plan_commit" "$original_plan_parent" \
        'docs: harden Task 4.2 execution plan' "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_commit "$implementation_commit" "$original_plan_commit" \
        'feat: settle passive initial state' "$implementation_scope" || return 1
    require_exact_commit "$design_commit" "$implementation_commit" \
        'docs: design Task 4.2 review correction' "$design_path" || return 1
    require_exact_commit "$r01_anchor" "$design_commit" \
        'docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r01' "$plan_scope" || return 1
    require_exact_commit "$r02_plan_anchor" "$r01_anchor" \
        'docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r02' "$plan_scope" || return 1
    require_exact_commit "$r02_tip" "$r02_plan_anchor" \
        'test: correct Task 4.2 behavioral evidence r02' "$test_scope" || return 1
    require_exact_commit "$r03_plan_anchor" "$r02_tip" \
        'docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r03' "$plan_scope" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r01_legacy_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r01_anchor" "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r01_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r01_anchor" "$plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r01_driver_sha" sha256_driver_blob "$r01_anchor" \
        '# TASK42_CORRECTION_R01_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
        '# TASK42_CORRECTION_R01_DRIVER_END' || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_legacy_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r02_plan_anchor" "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r02_plan_anchor" "$plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_driver_sha" sha256_driver_blob "$r02_plan_anchor" \
        '# TASK42_CORRECTION_R02_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
        '# TASK42_CORRECTION_R02_DRIVER_END' || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_legacy_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r02_tip" "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r02_tip" "$plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r03_legacy_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r03_plan_anchor" "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r03_plan_sha" \
        sha256_blob "$r03_plan_anchor" "$plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r03_driver_sha" sha256_driver_blob \
        "$r03_plan_anchor" '# TASK42_CORRECTION_R03_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
        '# TASK42_CORRECTION_R03_DRIVER_END' || return 1
    require_plan_anchor || return 1
}

require_seal_commit() {
    seal_commit="$1"
    require_exact_commit "$seal_commit" "$plan_anchor" \
        'docs: seal Task 4.2 correction evidence' "$seal_path" || return 1
}

require_production_hashes() {
    require_regular_sha "$driver_path" "$driver_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$boundaries_path" "$boundaries_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$program_path" "$program_sha" || return 1
}

require_corrected_tests() {
    local count_value
    require_regular_sha "$initial_tests_path" "$initial_post_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$support_path" "$support_post_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1745 file_lines "$initial_tests_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 60511 file_bytes "$initial_tests_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 475 file_lines "$support_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 13008 file_bytes "$support_path" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$oracle_install_tests_path" \
        "$oracle_install_tests_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output 7543 file_lines "$oracle_install_tests_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 255854 file_bytes "$oracle_install_tests_path" || return 1
    count_value="$(awk 'index($0,"tests.Add(\"driver-initial\"") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$initial_tests_path")" || return 1
    test "$count_value" = 8 || return 1
    count_value="$(awk 'index($0,"{ \"driver-initial\", 8 }") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$program_path")" || return 1
    test "$count_value" = 1 || return 1
    count_value="$(awk 'index($0,"SetNeutralSeenForDefensiveTest") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$initial_tests_path" "$support_path")" || return 1
    test "$count_value" = 0 || return 1
    count_value="$(awk 'index($0,"AssertCandidateClearedForDefensiveTest") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$initial_tests_path" "$support_path")" || return 1
    test "$count_value" = 0 || return 1
    count_value="$(awk 'index($0,"SetCurrentFramesForDefensiveTest") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$initial_tests_path" "$support_path")" || return 1
    test "$count_value" -ge 2 || return 1
    count_value="$(awk 'index($0,"ReadUpdateStage") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$initial_tests_path" "$support_path")" || return 1
    test "$count_value" -ge 2 || return 1
}

require_round_baseline() {
    require_exact_output "$plan_anchor" git rev-parse HEAD || return 1
    require_git_clean || return 1
    require_production_hashes || return 1
    require_corrected_tests || return 1
}

mutation_meta() {
    mutation_id="$1"
    case "$mutation_id" in
        01) patch_sha='21ef523b3dba38f96fd5c584476f77d7f3271a1680a274dbe23bb3629996dbca'; registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='any two captures do not settle'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        02) patch_sha='aec9fab4f5f3c63d571e99bc43c8eb8548ef827b8f0cda89c3bbb404b653dfef'; registration='pre epoch neutral does not leak'; fragment='new epoch neutral enables pair'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        03) patch_sha='e305e74f96d0f020c1972179076da6d20972a011bfea101d92effcb70a9fc299'; registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement requires two fresh candidate captures'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        04) patch_sha='ec925982835f5118007d8bf5295b15f25b9551acbe9bf3c95ca559344808af14'; registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='nonquiescent sample cleared candidate'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        05) patch_sha='c246ed1d26009cddcd5dbf531df79b87e7903838d72798a89874f4d2b565eef9'; registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='cardinal input cleared candidate'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        06) patch_sha='53c1333235d5bd2a23a3a13fc3eaa301ab642d87625ca6f1f429e2b689ae213d'; registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement neutral does not leak to next callback'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        07) patch_sha='c8be35f1a270e25196a15b331c7eba53697960921612171b2223699a36b48ce4'; registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement callback is frame one'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        08) patch_sha='fd978d8c67f328f782d0dbf1b38e1ef5e0fe447ef31ae45c9ca247237e291d21'; registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='new epoch pair settles inside reset deadline'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        09) patch_sha='5e94c29c919c0a5aed45f4d987f96076a20a9372621d69caee69dca015696c8d'; registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement cleared old last_capture'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        10) patch_sha='3b0e8b6b016b76e19bce61071e0ce38868d720fed3a08bd042d40912a772a382'; registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement directive uses new epoch'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        11) patch_sha='d05715b2bd91b1ca56cd8ad2b0956c85d248504d6b44323fdae5f14a0025f20d'; registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='nonquiescent callback cannot capture'; target_path="$boundaries_path" ;;
        12) patch_sha='23a8226a2fb1aee99c8a2d6997617b5c678b97ebff1f9fb7c6c21cf7032453d8'; registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='false path skips gate'; target_path="$boundaries_path" ;;
        13) patch_sha='958d789ae12dd27ecfbbde0a7f52fdf79b21048324c495fcadaea34517e4e787'; registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='unsuccessful exact frame times out phase'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        14) patch_sha='318186362020b0f44ff00dc713abc327d5edaea763997f94341023d741d1c386'; registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='frame pre-overrun precedes path'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        15) patch_sha='31e306733de78887c537415f1029dd578db0c40f00e78927af243f05cbb43fe3'; registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='frame pre-overrun is capped'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        16) patch_sha='f5dea294c9aa1acece61015c325889bbcf63735c3fabedf92ef4559a04e245e5'; registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='capture exception Error code'; target_path="$boundaries_path" ;;
        17) patch_sha='37acce7f3ae4ca2d7c278dc28a90afbd2149dc7ba41157c8e20069b819d78b06'; registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='Initial flush precedes Ready'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        18) patch_sha='f28b89acbbc86a0fab531365ca19e24918feee9f02475afd20b37dd99a8ca7f0'; registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='Ready failure follows durable Initial at index 9'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        19) patch_sha='e2624bfa3e88a6734e8d680b980c7b6b348f64050d7eb56c407d21f12196c52b'; registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='repeated observer failure writes one Error'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;

        21) patch_sha='64d1d47226c877985bb53481766ff7b02cef8f94e10af126a8d38ba99c9d583d'; registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='Error write failure uses trace marker'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        22) patch_sha='1673cf1540dd5b329af4c69c56a3a74aff775261e8f3ccf9a0012c5643a48e1f'; registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='cardinal input cleared neutral eligibility'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        23) patch_sha='3bfcb08286eeb52161905106a175c1268e751f011aea3b1241de37f532ce367a'; registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='same raw save with unequal complete signature does not settle'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        24) patch_sha='ae946c4df01fcb979c39ed5af9b8d4757a715079473bdf73556a7d1a6be23945'; registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='authorization precedes gate inspection'; target_path="$boundaries_path" ;;
        25) patch_sha='44d54cc4211878cff5b5072e316f999ea15cb389a7f3df08938a07c995185e83'; registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='Initial trace failure retains close ownership after Dispose'; target_path="$driver_path" ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    patch_path="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-M$mutation_id.patch"
}

manifest_paths() {
    local mutation_ids
    printf '%s\n' "$brief_path" || return 1
    mutation_ids="$(jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-M%s.patch\n' \
            "$artifact_dir" "$n" || return 1
    done
    for n in 03 07 10; do
        printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-red-M%s-output.txt\n' \
            "$artifact_dir" "$n" || return 1
        printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-red-M%s-status.txt\n' \
            "$artifact_dir" "$n" || return 1
    done
    for evidence_role in implementer controller; do
        for n in $mutation_ids; do
            printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-%s-M%s-output.txt\n' \
                "$artifact_dir" "$evidence_role" "$n" || return 1
            printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-%s-M%s-status.txt\n' \
                "$artifact_dir" "$evidence_role" "$n" || return 1
        done
    done
    printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt\n' \
        "$artifact_dir" || return 1
    printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt\n' \
        "$artifact_dir" || return 1
}

actual_round_paths() {
    for candidate in "$artifact_dir"/*; do
        if [ ! -e "$candidate" ] && [ ! -L "$candidate" ]; then
            continue
        fi
        candidate_name="$(basename "$candidate")" || return 1
        case "$candidate_name" in
            task-4.2-correction-r04-*|task-4.2-review-correction-r04-brief.md)
                printf '%s\n' "$candidate" || return 1
                ;;
        esac
    done | LC_ALL=C sort
}

    for candidate in "$artifact_dir"/*; do
        if [ ! -e "$candidate" ] && [ ! -L "$candidate" ]; then

verify_r01_manifest() {
    local actual_manifest
    local expected_manifest
    local manifest_count
    local materialized_manifest
    local ordinal
    expected_manifest="$(r01_manifest_paths)" || return 1
    manifest_count="$(printf '%s\n' "$expected_manifest" |
        wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
    test "$manifest_count" = 134 || return 1
    ordinal=0
    while IFS= read -r artifact_path; do
        ordinal=$((ordinal + 1))
        if [ "$ordinal" -le 26 ]; then
            test -f "$artifact_path" || return 1
            test ! -L "$artifact_path" || return 1
        else
            require_absent "$artifact_path" || return 1
        fi
    done <<< "$expected_manifest"
    test "$ordinal" = 134 || return 1
    actual_manifest="$(actual_r01_round_paths)" || return 1
    materialized_manifest="$(printf '%s\n' "$expected_manifest" |
        sed -n '1,26p' | LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1
    test "$actual_manifest" = "$materialized_manifest" || return 1
}

verify_manifest() {
    local actual_manifest
    local expected_manifest
    local manifest_count
    local materialized
    local materialized_manifest
    local ordinal
    evidence_pairs="$1"
    test "$evidence_pairs" -ge 0 || return 1
    test "$evidence_pairs" -le 54 || return 1
    materialized=$((26 + 2 * evidence_pairs))
    expected_manifest="$(manifest_paths)" || return 1
    manifest_count="$(printf '%s\n' "$expected_manifest" |
        wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
    test "$manifest_count" = 134 || return 1
    ordinal=0
    while IFS= read -r artifact_path; do
        ordinal=$((ordinal + 1))
        if [ "$ordinal" -le "$materialized" ]; then
            test -f "$artifact_path" || return 1
            test ! -L "$artifact_path" || return 1
        else
            require_absent "$artifact_path" || return 1
        fi
    done <<< "$expected_manifest"
    test "$ordinal" = 134 || return 1
    actual_manifest="$(actual_round_paths)" || return 1
    materialized_manifest="$(printf '%s\n' "$expected_manifest" |
        sed -n "1,${materialized}p" | LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1
    test "$actual_manifest" = "$materialized_manifest" || return 1
}

prefix_sha256() {
    dd if="$1" bs=1 count="$2" 2>/dev/null |
        shasum -a 256 | awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }'
}

require_ledger_prefixes() {
    local alias_status
    local current_bytes
    local measured_value
    local report_suffix_base64
    local progress_suffix_base64
    test -f "$report_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$report_path" || return 1
    test -f "$progress_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$progress_path" || return 1
    if test "$report_path" -ef "$progress_path"; then
        return 1
    else
        alias_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$alias_status" = 1 || return 1
    current_bytes="$(file_bytes "$report_path")" || return 1
    test "$current_bytes" -ge "$report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    current_bytes="$(file_bytes "$progress_path")" || return 1
    test "$current_bytes" -ge "$progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$report_anchor_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$report_path" "$report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$progress_anchor_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$progress_path" "$progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$historical_report_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$report_path" "$historical_report_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$historical_progress_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$progress_path" "$historical_progress_bytes" || return 1
    test "$((historical_report_bytes + r01_suffix_bytes))" = \
        "$r02_report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    test "$((historical_progress_bytes + r01_suffix_bytes))" = \
        "$r02_progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    test "$((r02_report_anchor_bytes + r02_suffix_bytes))" = \
        "$r03_report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    test "$((r02_progress_anchor_bytes + r02_suffix_bytes))" = \
        "$r03_progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    test "$((r03_report_anchor_bytes + r03_suffix_bytes))" = \
        "$report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    test "$((r03_progress_anchor_bytes + r03_suffix_bytes))" = \
        "$progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_report_anchor_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$report_path" "$r02_report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r02_progress_anchor_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$progress_path" "$r02_progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r03_report_anchor_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$report_path" "$r03_report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$r03_progress_anchor_sha" prefix_sha256 \
        "$progress_path" "$r03_progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip="$historical_report_bytes" \
        count="$r01_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null | shasum -a 256 |
        awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = "$r01_suffix_sha" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip="$historical_progress_bytes" \
        count="$r01_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null | shasum -a 256 |
        awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = "$r01_suffix_sha" || return 1
    report_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$historical_report_bytes" count="$r01_suffix_bytes" \
        2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    progress_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$historical_progress_bytes" count="$r01_suffix_bytes" \
        2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    test "$report_suffix_base64" = "$progress_suffix_base64" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip="$historical_report_bytes" \
        count="$r01_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null |
        awk 'index($0,"Task 4.2 correction r01 ") == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 44 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip="$historical_progress_bytes" \
        count="$r01_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null |
        awk 'index($0,"Task 4.2 correction r01 ") == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 44 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip="$r02_report_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r02_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null | shasum -a 256 |
        awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = "$r02_suffix_sha" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip="$r02_progress_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r02_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null | shasum -a 256 |
        awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = "$r02_suffix_sha" || return 1
    report_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$r02_report_anchor_bytes" count="$r02_suffix_bytes" \
        2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    progress_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$r02_progress_anchor_bytes" count="$r02_suffix_bytes" \
        2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    test "$report_suffix_base64" = "$progress_suffix_base64" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip="$r02_report_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r02_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null |
        awk 'index($0,"Task 4.2 correction r02 ") == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 80 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip="$r02_progress_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r02_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null |
        awk 'index($0,"Task 4.2 correction r02 ") == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 80 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip="$r03_report_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r03_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null | shasum -a 256 |
        awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = "$r03_suffix_sha" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip="$r03_progress_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r03_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null | shasum -a 256 |
        awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = "$r03_suffix_sha" || return 1
    report_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$r03_report_anchor_bytes" count="$r03_suffix_bytes" \
        2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    progress_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$r03_progress_anchor_bytes" count="$r03_suffix_bytes" \
        2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    test "$report_suffix_base64" = "$progress_suffix_base64" || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip="$r03_report_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r03_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null |
        awk 'index($0,"Task 4.2 correction r03 ") == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 110 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip="$r03_progress_anchor_bytes" \
        count="$r03_suffix_bytes" 2>/dev/null |
        awk 'index($0,"Task 4.2 correction r03 ") == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 110 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip=$((report_anchor_bytes - 1)) \
        count=1 2>/dev/null | od -An -tu1 | tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 10 || return 1
    measured_value="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip=$((progress_anchor_bytes - 1)) \
        count=1 2>/dev/null | od -An -tu1 | tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
    test "$measured_value" = 10 || return 1
}

key_count() {
    awk -v p="$row_prefix $2: " 'index($0,p) == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$1"
}

key_value() {
    awk -v p="$row_prefix $2: " 'index($0,p) == 1 { print substr($0,length(p)+1) }' "$1"
}

require_pair_value() {
    key="$1"
    expected_value="$2"
    require_exact_output 1 key_count "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 key_count "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_value" key_value "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_value" key_value "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
}

r02_key_count() {
    awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r02 $2: " \
        'index($0,p) == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$1"
}

r02_key_value() {
    awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r02 $2: " \
        'index($0,p) == 1 { print substr($0,length(p)+1) }' "$1"
}

require_r02_pair_value() {
    key="$1"
    expected_value="$2"
    require_exact_output 1 r02_key_count "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 r02_key_count "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
        r02_key_value "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
        r02_key_value "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
}

r03_key_count() {
    awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r03 $2: " \
        'index($0,p) == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$1"
}

r03_key_value() {
    awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r03 $2: " \
        'index($0,p) == 1 { print substr($0,length(p)+1) }' "$1"
}

require_r03_pair_value() {
    key="$1"
    expected_value="$2"
    require_exact_output 1 r03_key_count "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 r03_key_count "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
        r03_key_value "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
        r03_key_value "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
}

emit_row() {
    printf '%s %s: %s\n' "$row_prefix" "$1" "$2"
}

suite_value() {
    printf '%s' "helper-sha256=$helper_sha|unit=PASS|net35=PASS|focused=PASS|full=PASS|python=PASS|python-summary=$suite_python_summary"
}

require_static_artifacts() {
    local anchor_plan_sha
    local working_plan_sha
    local ignored_paths
    local mutation_ids
    require_regular_sha "$design_path" "$design_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$historical_design_path" "$historical_design_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$legacy_plan_path" "$legacy_plan_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output 28913 file_lines "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1132724 file_bytes "$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    anchor_plan_sha="$(sha256_blob "$plan_anchor" "$plan_path")" || return 1
    working_plan_sha="$(sha256_file "$plan_path")" || return 1
    test "$anchor_plan_sha" = "$working_plan_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$preseal_core_lines" \
        preseal_core_blob_lines "$plan_anchor" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$preseal_core_bytes" \
        preseal_core_blob_bytes "$plan_anchor" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$preseal_core_sha" \
        preseal_core_blob_sha "$plan_anchor" || return 1
    emit_preseal_core_blob "$plan_anchor" | /bin/bash -n || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$brief_path" "$brief_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$r01_brief_path" "$r01_brief_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$r02_brief_path" "$r02_brief_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$r03_brief_path" "$r03_brief_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$historical_brief_path" "$historical_brief_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output 202 file_lines "$brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 10876 file_bytes "$brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 188 file_lines "$r01_brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 11094 file_bytes "$r01_brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 198 file_lines "$r02_brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 11728 file_bytes "$r02_brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 190 file_lines "$r03_brief_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 12293 file_bytes "$r03_brief_path" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$suite_path" "$helper_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output 2858 file_bytes "$suite_path" || return 1
    require_production_hashes || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$ignore_path" "$ignore_sha" || return 1
    ignored_paths="$(manifest_paths)" || return 1
    while IFS= read -r ignored_path; do
        git check-ignore -q -- "$ignored_path" || return 1
    done <<< "$ignored_paths"
    ignored_paths="$(r01_manifest_paths)" || return 1
    while IFS= read -r ignored_path; do
        git check-ignore -q -- "$ignored_path" || return 1
    done <<< "$ignored_paths"
    ignored_paths="$(r02_manifest_paths)" || return 1
    while IFS= read -r ignored_path; do
        git check-ignore -q -- "$ignored_path" || return 1
    done <<< "$ignored_paths"
    ignored_paths="$(r03_manifest_paths)" || return 1
    while IFS= read -r ignored_path; do
        git check-ignore -q -- "$ignored_path" || return 1
    done <<< "$ignored_paths"
    git check-ignore -q -- "$report_path" || return 1
    git check-ignore -q -- "$progress_path" || return 1
    git check-ignore -q -- "$suite_path" || return 1
    mutation_ids="$(jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        mutation_meta "$n" || return 1
        require_regular_sha "$patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
        git apply --check "$patch_path" || return 1
        r01_patch_path="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r01-M$n.patch"
        r02_patch_path="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r02-M$n.patch"
        r03_patch_path="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r03-M$n.patch"
        require_regular_sha "$r01_patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
        require_regular_sha "$r02_patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
        require_regular_sha "$r03_patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
        cmp -s "$patch_path" "$r03_patch_path" || return 1
    done
    require_ledger_prefixes || return 1
    verify_r01_manifest || return 1
    verify_r02_manifest || return 1
    verify_r03_manifest || return 1
    for n in 03 07 10; do
        validate_r02_failure_pair red "$n" || return 1
    done
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        validate_r02_failure_pair implementer "$n" || return 1
    done
    for n in 03 07 10; do
        validate_r03_failure_pair red "$n" || return 1
    done
    for evidence_role in implementer controller; do
        for n in $mutation_ids; do
            validate_r03_failure_pair "$evidence_role" "$n" || return 1
        done
    done
    require_r03_pair_value 'plan anchor commit' \
        "commit=$r03_plan_anchor|parent=$r02_tip|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r03|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'test topology' \
        "inherited-test-commit=$r02_tip|parent=$r02_plan_anchor|subject=test: correct Task 4.2 behavioral evidence r02|scope=$initial_tests_path,$support_path|new-test-commit=ABSENT|gate-only=true" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'round tip' \
        "commit=$r03_plan_anchor|parent=$r02_tip|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r03|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'final preseal correction commit' \
        "commit=$r03_plan_anchor|parent=$r02_tip|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r03|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'descendant chain' \
        "$implementation_commit>$design_commit>$r01_anchor>$r02_plan_anchor>$r02_tip>$r03_plan_anchor" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'review specification' \
        "identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_specification_review|tip=$r03_plan_anchor|verdict=APPROVE|critical=0|important=0" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'review behavioral mutation' \
        "identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_behavioral_mutation_review|tip=$r03_plan_anchor|verdict=APPROVE|critical=0|important=0" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'review shell gate' \
        "identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_shell_gate_review|tip=$r03_plan_anchor|verdict=APPROVE|critical=0|important=0" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'review compatibility' \
        "identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_compatibility_review|tip=$r03_plan_anchor|verdict=APPROVE|critical=0|important=0" || return 1
    require_r03_pair_value 'controller replay summary' \
        "tip=$r03_plan_anchor|replays=25|killed=25|restored=25|focused-green=25" || return 1
    require_exact_output 0 r03_key_count "$report_path" 'preseal evidence' || return 1
    require_exact_output 0 r03_key_count "$progress_path" 'preseal evidence' || return 1
    require_exact_output 0 r03_key_count "$report_path" 'preseal full suite' || return 1
    require_exact_output 0 r03_key_count "$progress_path" 'preseal full suite' || return 1
}

emit_static_suffix() {
    local current_plan_sha
    local mutation_ids
    current_plan_sha="$(sha256_blob "$plan_anchor" "$plan_path")" || return 1
    emit_row 'original execution base' "$original_plan_commit" || return 1
    emit_row 'original plan commit' "commit=$original_plan_commit|parent=$original_plan_parent|subject=docs: harden Task 4.2 execution plan|scope=$legacy_plan_path" || return 1
    emit_row 'immutable implementation commit' "commit=$implementation_commit|parent=$original_plan_commit|subject=feat: settle passive initial state|scope=$driver_path,$boundaries_path,$initial_tests_path,$support_path,$program_path" || return 1
    emit_row 'correction design commit' "commit=$design_commit|parent=$implementation_commit|subject=docs: design Task 4.2 review correction|scope=$design_path" || return 1
    emit_row 'plan anchor commit' "commit=$plan_anchor|parent=$round_parent|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1
    emit_row 'historical report prefix' "path=$report_path|bytes=$historical_report_bytes|sha256=$historical_report_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'historical progress prefix' "path=$progress_path|bytes=$historical_progress_bytes|sha256=$historical_progress_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'anchor report prefix' "path=$report_path|bytes=$report_anchor_bytes|sha256=$report_anchor_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'anchor progress prefix' "path=$progress_path|bytes=$progress_anchor_bytes|sha256=$progress_anchor_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'design artifact' "path=$design_path|sha256=$design_sha|historical-path=$historical_design_path|historical-sha256=$historical_design_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'supersession plan artifact' "path=$legacy_plan_path|sha256=$legacy_plan_sha|rejected-r01-sha256=$r01_legacy_plan_sha|rejected-r02-sha256=$r02_legacy_plan_sha|rejected-r03-sha256=$r03_legacy_plan_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'correction plan artifact' "path=$plan_path|sha256=$current_plan_sha|rejected-r01-anchor=$r01_anchor|rejected-r01-sha256=$r01_plan_sha|rejected-r01-driver-sha256=$r01_driver_sha|rejected-r01-failure=baseline-shell-brace-expansion|rejected-r02-anchor=$r02_plan_anchor|rejected-r02-tip=$r02_tip|rejected-r02-sha256=$r02_plan_sha|rejected-r02-driver-sha256=$r02_driver_sha|rejected-r02-failure=preseal-manifest-ordering|rejected-r03-anchor=$r03_plan_anchor|rejected-r03-sha256=$r03_plan_sha|rejected-r03-driver-sha256=$r03_driver_sha|rejected-r03-failure=preseal-helper-umask-leak" || return 1
    emit_row 'correction brief artifact' "path=$brief_path|sha256=$brief_sha|historical-path=$historical_brief_path|historical-sha256=$historical_brief_sha|rejected-r01-path=$r01_brief_path|rejected-r01-sha256=$r01_brief_sha|rejected-r02-path=$r02_brief_path|rejected-r02-sha256=$r02_brief_sha|rejected-r03-path=$r03_brief_path|rejected-r03-sha256=$r03_brief_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'shared helper artifact' "path=$suite_path|sha256=$helper_sha|ignore-path=$ignore_path|ignore-sha256=$ignore_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'PassiveDriver artifact' "path=$driver_path|sha256=$driver_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'PassiveDriverBoundaries artifact' "path=$boundaries_path|sha256=$boundaries_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'Program artifact' "path=$program_path|sha256=$program_sha" || return 1
    emit_row 'PassiveDriverInitialTests artifact' "path=$initial_tests_path|pre-sha256=$initial_pre_sha|corrected-sha256=$initial_post_sha|corrected-lines=1745|corrected-bytes=60511" || return 1
    emit_row 'PassiveDriverTestSupport artifact' "path=$support_path|pre-sha256=$support_pre_sha|corrected-sha256=$support_post_sha|corrected-lines=475|corrected-bytes=13008" || return 1
    mutation_ids="$(jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        mutation_meta "$n" || return 1
        require_regular_sha "$patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
        emit_row "patch M$n artifact" "path=$patch_path|sha256=$patch_sha" || return 1
    done
}

evidence_paths() {
    evidence_role="$1"
    mutation_id="$2"
    evidence_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-output.txt"
    evidence_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-status.txt"
}

reject_failure_infrastructure() {
    local diagnostic_scan_status
    evidence_output="$1"
    if awk '
        /: error / || /: warning / || /error CS/ || /warning CS/ ||
        /Build FAILED[.]/ || /NETSDK/ || /assets file/ || /SDK/ ||
        /MSB[0-9]/ || /permission denied/ || /Permission denied/ ||
        /Operation not permitted/ || /UnauthorizedAccessException/ ||
        /timed out/ || /timeout/ || /NU1/ || /package/ || /Package/ ||
        /restore/ || /No such file/ || /command not found/ { bad=1 }
        END { exit bad ? 0 : 1 }
    ' "$evidence_output"; then
        return 1
    else
        diagnostic_scan_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$diagnostic_scan_status" = 1 || return 1
}

validate_failure_pair() {
    evidence_role="$1"
    mutation_id="$2"
    mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
    evidence_paths "$evidence_role" "$mutation_id" || return 1
    marker="$row_prefix $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
    test -f "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test ! -L "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test -s "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test -f "$evidence_status" || return 1
    test ! -L "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 file_lines "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 sed -n '1p' "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$status_one_sha" \
        sha256_file "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$marker" sed -n '1p' "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '$0 == "Build succeeded." { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Warning[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Error[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^cohort / { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    failure_line="$(awk '/^cohort / { print; exit }' "$evidence_output")" || return 1
    failure_prefix="cohort 'driver-initial', test '$registration': System.InvalidOperationException: "
    case "$failure_line" in
        "$failure_prefix"*"$fragment"*) ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    reject_failure_infrastructure "$evidence_output" || return 1
}

validate_r02_failure_pair() {
    local historical_output_sha
    local historical_status_sha
    evidence_role="$1"
    mutation_id="$2"
    mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
    evidence_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r02-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-output.txt"
    evidence_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r02-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-status.txt"
    marker="Task 4.2 correction r02 $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
    test -f "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test ! -L "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test -s "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test -f "$evidence_status" || return 1
    test ! -L "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 file_lines "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 sed -n '1p' "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$status_one_sha" \
        sha256_file "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$marker" sed -n '1p' "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '$0 == "Build succeeded." { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Warning[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Error[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^cohort / { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    failure_line="$(awk '/^cohort / { print; exit }' "$evidence_output")" || return 1
    failure_prefix="cohort 'driver-initial', test '$registration': System.InvalidOperationException: "
    case "$failure_line" in
        "$failure_prefix"*"$fragment"*) ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    reject_failure_infrastructure "$evidence_output" || return 1
    historical_output_sha="$(sha256_file "$evidence_output")" || return 1
    historical_status_sha="$(sha256_file "$evidence_status")" || return 1
    historical_value="output-path=$evidence_output|output-sha256=$historical_output_sha|status-path=$evidence_status|status-sha256=$historical_status_sha|marker=$marker|registration=$registration|fragment=$fragment|status=1|result=KILLED|restoration=IMPLEMENTER_BASELINE|focused-green=PASS"
    require_r02_pair_value \
        "$evidence_role M$mutation_id evidence" "$historical_value" || return 1
}

validate_r03_failure_pair() {
    local historical_output_sha
    local historical_status_sha
    evidence_role="$1"
    mutation_id="$2"
    mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
    evidence_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r03-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-output.txt"
    evidence_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r03-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-status.txt"
    marker="Task 4.2 correction r03 $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
    test -f "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test ! -L "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test -s "$evidence_output" || return 1
    test -f "$evidence_status" || return 1
    test ! -L "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 file_lines "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 sed -n '1p' "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$status_one_sha" \
        sha256_file "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$marker" sed -n '1p' "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '$0 == "Build succeeded." { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Warning[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Error[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        '/^cohort / { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$evidence_output" || return 1
    failure_line="$(awk '/^cohort / { print; exit }' \
        "$evidence_output")" || return 1
    failure_prefix="cohort 'driver-initial', test '$registration': System.InvalidOperationException: "
    case "$failure_line" in
        "$failure_prefix"*"$fragment"*) ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    reject_failure_infrastructure "$evidence_output" || return 1
    historical_output_sha="$(sha256_file "$evidence_output")" || return 1
    historical_status_sha="$(sha256_file "$evidence_status")" || return 1
    historical_value="output-path=$evidence_output|output-sha256=$historical_output_sha|status-path=$evidence_status|status-sha256=$historical_status_sha|marker=$marker|registration=$registration|fragment=$fragment|status=1|result=KILLED|restoration=GIT_CLEAN|focused-green=PASS"
    require_r03_pair_value \
        "$evidence_role M$mutation_id evidence" "$historical_value" || return 1
}

evidence_value() {
    local evidence_output_sha
    local evidence_status_sha
    evidence_role="$1"
    mutation_id="$2"
    restoration="$3"
    mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
    evidence_paths "$evidence_role" "$mutation_id" || return 1
    validate_failure_pair "$evidence_role" "$mutation_id" || return 1
    marker="$row_prefix $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
    evidence_output_sha="$(sha256_file "$evidence_output")" || return 1
    evidence_status_sha="$(sha256_file "$evidence_status")" || return 1
    printf '%s' "output-path=$evidence_output|output-sha256=$evidence_output_sha|status-path=$evidence_status|status-sha256=$evidence_status_sha|marker=$marker|registration=$registration|fragment=$fragment|status=1|result=KILLED|restoration=$restoration|focused-green=PASS" || return 1
}

# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_BEGIN
preseal_predicate='none'

require_owned_regular_mode() {
    local expected_mode
    local file_link_count
    local file_mode
    local file_uid
    local owned_path
    local process_uid
    owned_path="$1"
    expected_mode="$2"
    test -f "$owned_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$owned_path" || return 1
    file_uid="$(stat -f '%u' "$owned_path")" || return 1
    process_uid="$(id -u)" || return 1
    test "$file_uid" = "$process_uid" || return 1
    file_mode="$(stat -f '%Lp' "$owned_path")" || return 1
    test "$file_mode" = "$expected_mode" || return 1
    file_link_count="$(stat -f '%l' "$owned_path")" || return 1
    test "$file_link_count" = 1 || return 1
}

validate_preseal_pair_at() {
    local alias_status
    local preseal_output
    local preseal_scan_status
    local preseal_status
    preseal_output="$1"
    preseal_status="$2"
    preseal_predicate='none'
    require_owned_regular_mode "$preseal_output" 600 || \
        { preseal_predicate='output-authentication'; return 1; }
    test -s "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='output-nonempty'; return 1; }
    require_owned_regular_mode "$preseal_status" 600 || \
        { preseal_predicate='status-authentication'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 1 file_lines "$preseal_status" || \
        { preseal_predicate='status-line-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 0 sed -n '1p' "$preseal_status" || \
        { preseal_predicate='status-value'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output "$status_zero_sha" \
        sha256_file "$preseal_status" || \
        { preseal_predicate='status-sha256'; return 1; }
    if test "$preseal_output" -ef "$preseal_status"; then
        preseal_predicate='output-status-alias'
        return 1
    else
        alias_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$alias_status" = 1 || \
        { preseal_predicate='output-status-alias-check'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output "$row_prefix preseal" \
        sed -n '1p' "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='marker-first-line'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 1 awk -v marker="$row_prefix preseal" \
        '$0 == marker { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='marker-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 2 awk \
        '$0 == "Build succeeded." { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='build-success-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 2 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Warning[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='zero-warning-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 2 awk \
        '/^[[:space:]]*0 Error[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='zero-error-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 2 awk -v harness="$suite_harness_line" \
        '$0 == harness { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='harness-ready-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 1 awk -v summary="$suite_python_summary" \
        'index($0,summary " in ") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='python-summary-count'; return 1; }
    require_exact_output 0 awk \
        '/^cohort / { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$preseal_output" || \
        { preseal_predicate='cohort-output-present'; return 1; }
    if awk '
        /: error / || /: warning / || /error CS/ || /warning CS/ ||
        /Build FAILED[.]/ || /NETSDK/ || /assets file/ || /SDK/ || /MSB[0-9]/ ||
        /permission denied/ || /Permission denied/ ||
        /Operation not permitted/ || /UnauthorizedAccessException/ ||
        /timed out/ || /timeout/ || /NU1/ || / failed/ || / error/ ||
        / skipped/ || /package/ || /Package/ || /restore/ ||
        /No such file/ || /command not found/ { bad=1 }
        END { exit bad ? 0 : 1 }
    ' "$preseal_output"; then
        preseal_predicate='forbidden-diagnostic'
        return 1
    else
        preseal_scan_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$preseal_scan_status" = 1 || \
        { preseal_predicate='diagnostic-scan-status'; return 1; }
    preseal_predicate='none'
}

validate_preseal_pair() {
    canonical_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt"
    canonical_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt"
    validate_preseal_pair_at "$canonical_output" "$canonical_status" || return 1
}

preseal_value() {
    local preseal_output_sha
    local preseal_status_sha
    preseal_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt"
    preseal_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt"
    validate_preseal_pair || return 1
    preseal_output_sha="$(sha256_file "$preseal_output")" || return 1
    preseal_status_sha="$(sha256_file "$preseal_status")" || return 1
    printf '%s' "output-path=$preseal_output|output-sha256=$preseal_output_sha|status-path=$preseal_status|status-sha256=$preseal_status_sha|marker=$row_prefix preseal|status=0|result=PASS|full-suite=PASS" || return 1
}

review_identity() {
    review_slug="$1"
    case "$review_slug" in
        specification)
            review_key='review specification'
            reviewer='/root/task_4_2_r04_specification_review'
            ;;
        behavioral-mutation)
            review_key='review behavioral mutation'
            reviewer='/root/task_4_2_r04_behavioral_mutation_review'
            ;;
        shell-gate)
            review_key='review shell gate'
            reviewer='/root/task_4_2_r04_shell_gate_review'
            ;;
        compatibility)
            review_key='review compatibility'
            reviewer='/root/task_4_2_r04_compatibility_review'
            ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
}

review_value() {
    review_slug="$1"
    review_identity "$review_slug" || return 1
    printf '%s' "identity=$reviewer|tip=$plan_anchor|verdict=APPROVE|critical=0|important=0" || return 1
}

emit_generated_row() {
    local generated_key="$1"
    local generated_function="$2"
    local generated_value
    shift 2
    generated_value="$("$generated_function" "$@")" || return 1
    emit_row "$generated_key" "$generated_value" || return 1
}

emit_expected_suffix() {
    local mutation_ids
    suffix_rows="$1"
    if [ "$suffix_rows" != 0 ]; then
        test "$suffix_rows" -ge 44 || return 1
    fi
    test "$suffix_rows" -le 112 || return 1
    if [ "$suffix_rows" = 0 ]; then
        return 0
    fi
    emit_static_suffix || return 1
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 45 ]; then emit_generated_row 'red M03 evidence' evidence_value red 03 GIT_CLEAN || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 46 ]; then emit_generated_row 'red M07 evidence' evidence_value red 07 GIT_CLEAN || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 47 ]; then emit_generated_row 'red M10 evidence' evidence_value red 10 GIT_CLEAN || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 48 ]; then emit_generated_row 'post-RED full GREEN' suite_value || return 1; fi
    row_number=49
    mutation_ids="$(jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge "$row_number" ]; then
            emit_generated_row "implementer M$n evidence" \
                evidence_value implementer "$n" GIT_CLEAN || return 1
        fi
        row_number=$((row_number + 1))
    done
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 74 ]; then emit_generated_row 'clean P4 full suite one' suite_value || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 75 ]; then emit_generated_row 'clean P4 full suite two' suite_value || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 76 ]; then emit_row 'test topology' "inherited-test-commit=$r02_tip|parent=$r02_plan_anchor|subject=test: correct Task 4.2 behavioral evidence r02|scope=$initial_tests_path,$support_path|new-test-commit=ABSENT|gate-only=true" || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 77 ]; then emit_row 'round tip' "commit=$plan_anchor|parent=$round_parent|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 78 ]; then emit_row 'final preseal correction commit' "commit=$plan_anchor|parent=$round_parent|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 79 ]; then emit_row 'descendant chain' "$implementation_commit>$design_commit>$r01_anchor>$r02_plan_anchor>$r02_tip>$r03_plan_anchor>$plan_anchor" || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 80 ]; then emit_generated_row 'clean P4 full suite three' suite_value || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 81 ]; then emit_generated_row 'review specification' review_value specification || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 82 ]; then emit_generated_row 'review behavioral mutation' review_value behavioral-mutation || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 83 ]; then emit_generated_row 'review shell gate' review_value shell-gate || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 84 ]; then emit_generated_row 'review compatibility' review_value compatibility || return 1; fi
    row_number=85
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge "$row_number" ]; then
            emit_generated_row "controller M$n evidence" \
                evidence_value controller "$n" GIT_CLEAN || return 1
        fi
        row_number=$((row_number + 1))
    done
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 110 ]; then
        emit_row 'controller replay summary' "tip=$plan_anchor|replays=25|killed=25|restored=25|focused-green=25" || return 1
    fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 111 ]; then emit_generated_row 'preseal evidence' preseal_value || return 1; fi
    if [ "$suffix_rows" -ge 112 ]; then emit_generated_row 'preseal full suite' suite_value || return 1; fi
    return 0
}

verify_ledgers() {
    local expected_suffix_base64
    local progress_suffix_base64
    local report_size
    local report_suffix_base64
    local progress_size
    local suffix_rows
    local terminal_byte
    suffix_rows="$1"
    require_ledger_prefixes || return 1
    expected_suffix_base64="$(emit_expected_suffix "$suffix_rows" |
        /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    report_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$report_anchor_bytes" 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    progress_suffix_base64="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 \
        skip="$progress_anchor_bytes" 2>/dev/null | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
    test "$report_suffix_base64" = "$expected_suffix_base64" || return 1
    test "$progress_suffix_base64" = "$expected_suffix_base64" || return 1
    test "$report_suffix_base64" = "$progress_suffix_base64" || return 1
    if [ "$suffix_rows" = 0 ]; then
        require_exact_output "$report_anchor_bytes" \
            file_bytes "$report_path" || return 1
        require_exact_output "$progress_anchor_bytes" \
            file_bytes "$progress_path" || return 1
    else
        report_size="$(file_bytes "$report_path")" || return 1
        progress_size="$(file_bytes "$progress_path")" || return 1
        test "$report_size" -gt "$report_anchor_bytes" || return 1
        test "$progress_size" -gt "$progress_anchor_bytes" || return 1
        terminal_byte="$(dd if="$report_path" bs=1 skip=$((report_size - 1)) \
            count=1 2>/dev/null | od -An -tu1 | tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
        test "$terminal_byte" = 10 || return 1
        terminal_byte="$(dd if="$progress_path" bs=1 skip=$((progress_size - 1)) \
            count=1 2>/dev/null | od -An -tu1 | tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
        test "$terminal_byte" = 10 || return 1
    fi
    return 0
}

append_row_pair() {
    before_rows="$1"
    key="$2"
    value="$3"
    verify_ledgers "$before_rows" || return 1
    require_exact_output 0 key_count "$report_path" "$key" || return 1
    require_exact_output 0 key_count "$progress_path" "$key" || return 1
    printf '%s %s: %s\n' "$row_prefix" "$key" "$value" >> "$report_path" || return 1
    printf '%s %s: %s\n' "$row_prefix" "$key" "$value" >> "$progress_path" || return 1
    verify_ledgers "$((before_rows + 1))" || return 1
    require_pair_value "$key" "$value" || return 1
}

append_static_suffix() {
    verify_ledgers 0 || return 1
    emit_static_suffix >> "$report_path" || return 1
    emit_static_suffix >> "$progress_path" || return 1
    verify_ledgers 44 || return 1
}

require_helper_row() {
    require_regular_sha "$suite_path" "$helper_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$ignore_path" "$ignore_sha" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk -v summary="$suite_python_summary" \
        'index($0,summary) { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$suite_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 2 awk -v harness="$suite_harness_line" \
        'index($0,harness) { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$suite_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        'index($0,"run_capture '\''unit Rebuild'\''") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$suite_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 awk \
        'index($0,"run_capture '\''net35 Core Rebuild'\''") { n++ } END { print n+0 }' \
        "$suite_path" || return 1
    require_pair_value 'shared helper artifact' \
        "path=$suite_path|sha256=$helper_sha|ignore-path=$ignore_path|ignore-sha256=$ignore_sha" || return 1
}

run_full_suite() {
    local helper_status
    require_helper_row || return 1
    set +e
    (
        umask 022
        /bin/bash "$suite_path"
    )
    helper_status="$?"
    set -e
    test "$helper_status" = 0 || return 1
}

require_build_text() {
    local build_succeeded_count
    local build_warning_count
    local build_error_count
    build_text="$1"
    build_succeeded_count="$(printf '%s\n' "$build_text" |
        awk '$0 == "Build succeeded." { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$build_succeeded_count" = 1 || return 1
    build_warning_count="$(printf '%s\n' "$build_text" |
        awk '/^[[:space:]]*0 Warning[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$build_warning_count" = 1 || return 1
    build_error_count="$(printf '%s\n' "$build_text" |
        awk '/^[[:space:]]*0 Error[(]s[)]$/ { n++ } END { print n+0 }')" || return 1
    test "$build_error_count" = 1 || return 1
    case "$build_text" in
        *': error '*|*': warning '*|*'error CS'*|*'warning CS'*|*'Build FAILED.'*|*'NETSDK'*|*'assets file'*|*'MSB'*|*'permission denied'*|*'Permission denied'*|*'Operation not permitted'*|*'UnauthorizedAccessException'*|*'timed out'*|*'timeout'*|*'NU1'*|*'package'*|*'Package'*|*'restore'*) return 1 ;;
    esac
    return 0
}

run_focused_green() {
    set +e
    focused_build="$($dotnet_bin build "$unit_project" \
        --configuration Release --no-restore --nologo \
        -warnaserror -t:Rebuild -m:1 -p:UseSharedCompilation=false 2>&1)"
    focused_build_status="$?"
    set -e
    test "$focused_build_status" = 0 || return 1
    require_build_text "$focused_build" || return 1
    set +e
    focused_output="$($dotnet_bin run --project "$unit_project" \
        --configuration Release --no-restore --no-build -- \
        --cohort driver-initial 2>&1)"
    focused_status="$?"
    set -e
    test "$focused_status" = 0 || return 1
    test "$focused_output" = 'SSR oracle unit harness ready' || return 1
}

require_mutated_state() {
    local actual_mutated_scope
    local actual_mutated_sha
    evidence_role="$1"
    target_path="$2"
    require_empty_index || return 1
    require_no_nonignored_untracked || return 1
    if [ "$evidence_role" != red ] &&
       [ "$evidence_role" != implementer ] &&
       [ "$evidence_role" != controller ]; then
        return 1
    fi
    actual_mutated_scope="$(git diff --name-only | LC_ALL=C sort)" || return 1
    test "$actual_mutated_scope" = "$target_path" || return 1
    git diff --check || return 1
```
