```bash
    require_corrected_tests || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$program_path" "$program_sha" || return 1
    if [ "$target_path" = "$driver_path" ]; then
        actual_mutated_sha="$(sha256_file "$driver_path")" || return 1
        test "$actual_mutated_sha" != "$driver_sha" || return 1
        require_regular_sha "$boundaries_path" "$boundaries_sha" || return 1
    else
        test "$target_path" = "$boundaries_path" || return 1
        actual_mutated_sha="$(sha256_file "$boundaries_path")" || return 1
        test "$actual_mutated_sha" != "$boundaries_sha" || return 1
        require_regular_sha "$driver_path" "$driver_sha" || return 1
    fi
}

require_role_baseline() {
    evidence_role="$1"
    if [ "$evidence_role" != red ] &&
       [ "$evidence_role" != implementer ] &&
       [ "$evidence_role" != controller ]; then
        return 1
    fi
    require_round_baseline || return 1
}

patch_applied=0
patch_snapshot_dir=''
patch_snapshot=''

cleanup_patch_snapshot() {
    snapshot_cleanup_failed=0
    if [ -n "$patch_snapshot" ] &&
       { [ -e "$patch_snapshot" ] || [ -L "$patch_snapshot" ]; }; then
        if [ -f "$patch_snapshot" ] && [ ! -L "$patch_snapshot" ]; then
            /bin/rm -f "$patch_snapshot" || snapshot_cleanup_failed=1
        else
            snapshot_cleanup_failed=1
        fi
    fi
    if [ -n "$patch_snapshot_dir" ] &&
       { [ -e "$patch_snapshot_dir" ] || [ -L "$patch_snapshot_dir" ]; }; then
        if [ -d "$patch_snapshot_dir" ] && [ ! -L "$patch_snapshot_dir" ]; then
            /bin/rmdir "$patch_snapshot_dir" || snapshot_cleanup_failed=1
        else
            snapshot_cleanup_failed=1
        fi
    fi
    if [ "$snapshot_cleanup_failed" = 0 ]; then
        patch_snapshot=''
        patch_snapshot_dir=''
        return 0
    fi
    return 1
}

cleanup_patch_on_exit() {
    cleanup_status="$?"
    trap - EXIT
    trap '' HUP INT TERM
    set +e
    cleanup_failed=0
    inverse_completed=0
    if [ "$patch_applied" = 1 ]; then
        (
            set -euo pipefail
            require_regular_sha "$patch_snapshot" "$patch_sha"
        )
        snapshot_auth_status="$?"
        if [ "$snapshot_auth_status" = 0 ]; then
            git apply -R --check "$patch_snapshot"
            inverse_check_status="$?"
            if [ "$inverse_check_status" = 0 ]; then
                git apply -R "$patch_snapshot"
                inverse_status="$?"
                if [ "$inverse_status" != 0 ]; then
                    cleanup_failed=1
                else
                    inverse_completed=1
                fi
            else
                (
                    set -euo pipefail
                    require_role_baseline "$evidence_role"
                )
                already_restored_status="$?"
                if [ "$already_restored_status" = 0 ]; then
                    inverse_completed=1
                else
                    cleanup_failed=1
                fi
            fi
        else
            cleanup_failed=1
        fi
    fi
    (
        set -euo pipefail
        require_role_baseline "$evidence_role"
    )
    restored_baseline_status="$?"
    if [ "$restored_baseline_status" != 0 ]; then
        cleanup_failed=1
    elif [ "$patch_applied" = 1 ] && [ "$inverse_completed" = 1 ]; then
        patch_applied=0
    fi
    if [ "$cleanup_failed" = 0 ]; then
        cleanup_patch_snapshot
        snapshot_cleanup_status="$?"
        if [ "$snapshot_cleanup_status" != 0 ]; then
            cleanup_failed=1
        fi
    fi
    if [ "$cleanup_failed" != 0 ]; then
        printf '%s\n' \
            "Task 4.2 correction r04 cleanup failed; preserve snapshot directory: $patch_snapshot_dir" >&2
        if [ -f "$patch_snapshot" ] && [ ! -L "$patch_snapshot" ]; then
            preserved_snapshot_sha="$(sha256_file "$patch_snapshot")"
            preserved_snapshot_sha_status="$?"
            if [ "$preserved_snapshot_sha_status" = 0 ]; then
                printf '%s\n' \
                    "Task 4.2 correction r04 preserved patch snapshot sha256: $preserved_snapshot_sha" >&2
            else
                printf '%s\n' \
                    'Task 4.2 correction r04 preserved patch snapshot sha256 unavailable' >&2
            fi
        fi
        exit 126
    fi
    exit "$cleanup_status"
}

run_mutation() {
    local evidence_record
    evidence_role="$1"
    mutation_id="$2"
    before_rows="$3"
    before_pairs="$4"
    restoration="$5"
    mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
    evidence_paths "$evidence_role" "$mutation_id" || return 1
    marker="$row_prefix $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
    verify_ledgers "$before_rows" || return 1
    verify_manifest "$before_pairs" || return 1
    require_role_baseline "$evidence_role" || return 1
    require_absent "$evidence_output" || return 1
    require_absent "$evidence_status" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
    require_pair_value "patch M$mutation_id artifact" \
        "path=$patch_path|sha256=$patch_sha" || return 1
    patch_snapshot_dir="$(/usr/bin/mktemp -d \
        /private/tmp/task-4.2-correction-r04-patch.XXXXXX)" || return 1
    test -d "$patch_snapshot_dir" || return 1
    test ! -L "$patch_snapshot_dir" || return 1
    patch_snapshot="$patch_snapshot_dir/patch"
    trap cleanup_patch_on_exit EXIT
    trap 'exit 129' HUP
    trap 'exit 130' INT
    trap 'exit 143' TERM
    /bin/cp "$patch_path" "$patch_snapshot" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$patch_snapshot" "$patch_sha" || return 1
    git apply --check "$patch_snapshot" || return 1
    patch_applied=1
    git apply "$patch_snapshot" || return 1
    require_mutated_state "$evidence_role" "$target_path" || return 1
    set +e
    set -o noclobber
    (
        set -euo pipefail
        printf '%s\n' "$marker" || exit 1
        "$dotnet_bin" build "$unit_project" \
            --configuration Release --no-restore --nologo \
            -warnaserror -t:Rebuild -m:1 -p:UseSharedCompilation=false || exit "$?"
        "$dotnet_bin" run --project "$unit_project" \
            --configuration Release --no-restore --no-build -- \
            --cohort driver-initial || exit "$?"
    ) > "$evidence_output" 2>&1
    captured_status="$?"
    printf '%s\n' "$captured_status" > "$evidence_status"
    status_write_status="$?"
    set +o noclobber
    set -e
    require_regular_sha "$patch_path" "$patch_sha" || return 1
    require_regular_sha "$patch_snapshot" "$patch_sha" || return 1
    trap '' HUP INT TERM
    git apply -R --check "$patch_snapshot" || return 1
    git apply -R "$patch_snapshot" || return 1
    patch_applied=0
    trap 'exit 129' HUP
    trap 'exit 130' INT
    trap 'exit 143' TERM
    test "$status_write_status" = 0 || return 1
    require_role_baseline "$evidence_role" || return 1
    set +e
    cleanup_patch_snapshot
    normal_snapshot_cleanup_status="$?"
    set -e
    if [ "$normal_snapshot_cleanup_status" != 0 ]; then
        exit 126
    fi
    trap - EXIT HUP INT TERM
    run_focused_green || return 1
    validate_failure_pair "$evidence_role" "$mutation_id" || return 1
    evidence_record="$(evidence_value \
        "$evidence_role" "$mutation_id" "$restoration")" || return 1
    append_row_pair "$before_rows" \
        "$evidence_role M$mutation_id evidence" "$evidence_record" || return 1
    verify_manifest "$((before_pairs + 1))" || return 1
}

require_common() {
    require_fixed_history || return 1
    require_static_artifacts || return 1
}

mode_anchor() {
    local report_size
    local progress_size
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_exact_output "$plan_anchor" git rev-parse HEAD || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    verify_manifest 0 || return 1
    report_size="$(file_bytes "$report_path")" || return 1
    progress_size="$(file_bytes "$progress_path")" || return 1
    if [ "$report_size" = "$report_anchor_bytes" ] &&
       [ "$progress_size" = "$progress_anchor_bytes" ]; then
        append_static_suffix || return 1
    else
        verify_ledgers 44 || return 1
    fi
    verify_manifest 0 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
}

mode_baseline() {
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    verify_ledgers 44 || return 1
    verify_manifest 0 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
}

mode_red() {
    local suite_record
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    verify_ledgers 44 || return 1
    verify_manifest 0 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    before_rows=44
    before_pairs=0
    for n in 03 07 10; do
        run_mutation red "$n" "$before_rows" \
            "$before_pairs" GIT_CLEAN || return 1
        before_rows=$((before_rows + 1))
        before_pairs=$((before_pairs + 1))
    done
    verify_ledgers 47 || return 1
    verify_manifest 3 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    run_full_suite || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    suite_record="$(suite_value)" || return 1
    append_row_pair 47 'post-RED full GREEN' "$suite_record" || return 1
    verify_ledgers 48 || return 1
    verify_manifest 3 || return 1
}

mode_implementer() {
    local mutation_ids
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    verify_ledgers 48 || return 1
    verify_manifest 3 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    before_rows=48
    before_pairs=3
    mutation_ids="$(jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        run_mutation implementer "$n" "$before_rows" \
            "$before_pairs" GIT_CLEAN || return 1
        before_rows=$((before_rows + 1))
        before_pairs=$((before_pairs + 1))
    done
    verify_ledgers 73 || return 1
    verify_manifest 28 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
}

mode_gate_suites() {
    local suite_record
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    verify_ledgers 73 || return 1
    verify_manifest 28 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    run_full_suite || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    suite_record="$(suite_value)" || return 1
    append_row_pair 73 'clean P4 full suite one' "$suite_record" || return 1
    run_full_suite || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    suite_record="$(suite_value)" || return 1
    append_row_pair 74 'clean P4 full suite two' "$suite_record" || return 1
    append_row_pair 75 'test topology' "inherited-test-commit=$r02_tip|parent=$r02_plan_anchor|subject=test: correct Task 4.2 behavioral evidence r02|scope=$initial_tests_path,$support_path|new-test-commit=ABSENT|gate-only=true" || return 1
    append_row_pair 76 'round tip' "commit=$plan_anchor|parent=$round_parent|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1
    append_row_pair 77 'final preseal correction commit' "commit=$plan_anchor|parent=$round_parent|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r04|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path" || return 1
    append_row_pair 78 'descendant chain' "$implementation_commit>$design_commit>$r01_anchor>$r02_plan_anchor>$r02_tip>$r03_plan_anchor>$plan_anchor" || return 1
    run_full_suite || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    suite_record="$(suite_value)" || return 1
    append_row_pair 79 'clean P4 full suite three' "$suite_record" || return 1
    verify_ledgers 80 || return 1
    verify_manifest 28 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
}

mode_record_review() {
    local review_record
    test "$#" = 6 || return 1
    review_slug="$1"
    asserted_identity="$2"
    asserted_tip="$3"
    asserted_verdict="$4"
    asserted_critical="$5"
    asserted_important="$6"
    require_common || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    review_identity "$review_slug" || return 1
    test "$asserted_identity" = "$reviewer" || return 1
    test "$asserted_tip" = "$plan_anchor" || return 1
    test "$asserted_verdict" = APPROVE || return 1
    test "$asserted_critical" = 0 || return 1
    test "$asserted_important" = 0 || return 1
    case "$review_slug" in
        specification) before_rows=80 ;;
        behavioral-mutation) before_rows=81 ;;
        shell-gate) before_rows=82 ;;
        compatibility) before_rows=83 ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    verify_ledgers "$before_rows" || return 1
    verify_manifest 28 || return 1
    review_record="$(review_value "$review_slug")" || return 1
    append_row_pair "$before_rows" "$review_key" "$review_record" || return 1
}

mode_controller() {
    local mutation_ids
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    verify_ledgers 84 || return 1
    verify_manifest 28 || return 1
    before_rows=84
    before_pairs=28
    mutation_ids="$(jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
        run_mutation controller "$n" "$before_rows" \
            "$before_pairs" GIT_CLEAN || return 1
        before_rows=$((before_rows + 1))
        before_pairs=$((before_pairs + 1))
    done
    append_row_pair 109 'controller replay summary' \
        "tip=$plan_anchor|replays=25|killed=25|restored=25|focused-green=25" || return 1
    verify_ledgers 110 || return 1
    verify_manifest 53 || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
}

private_dir=''
private_diagnostic=''
private_output=''
private_status=''
private_active=0
private_bundle_complete=0
private_cleanup_committed=0
private_diag_fd_open=0
private_output_fd_open=0
private_status_fd_open=0
preseal_stage='setup'
preseal_helper_status='NOT-RUN'

preseal_harness_active() {
    test "${TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_HARNESS-}" = \
        'task-4.2-r04-synthetic-v1'
}

preseal_harness_point() {
    local hook_status
    local hook_type
    local point
    point="$1"
    if ! preseal_harness_active; then
        return 0
    fi
    test "${TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_POINT-}" = "$point" || return 0
    hook_type="$(builtin type -t task42_preseal_harness_hook)" || return 125
    test "$hook_type" = function || return 125
    if task42_preseal_harness_hook "$point"; then
        hook_status=0
    else
        hook_status="$?"
    fi
    return "$hook_status"
}

preseal_inject_or_fail() {
    local hook_status
    local point
    local stage_code
    stage_code="$1"
    point="$2"
    if preseal_harness_point "$point"; then
        return 0
    else
        hook_status="$?"
    fi
    preseal_record_failure "$stage_code" \
        "synthetic-$point-raw-$hook_status"
    return "$?"
}

preseal_private_template() {
    if preseal_harness_active; then
        case "${TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_PRIVATE_PREFIX-}" in
            /private/tmp/task-4.2-r04-core-matrix.??????/private-preseal) ;;
            *) return 80 ;;
        esac
        printf '%s.XXXXXX\n' "$TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_PRIVATE_PREFIX" || return 80
    else
        printf '%s\n' \
            '/private/tmp/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal.XXXXXX' || return 80
    fi
}

require_capture_fds_closed() {
    local fd_status
    if : 2>/dev/null >&3; then
        return 1
    else
        fd_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$fd_status" -ne 0 || return 1
    if : 2>/dev/null >&4; then
        return 1
    else
        fd_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$fd_status" -ne 0 || return 1
    if : 2>/dev/null >&5; then
        return 1
    else
        fd_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$fd_status" -ne 0 || return 1
}

authenticate_private_dir() {
    local canonical_private_dir
    local private_dir_mode
    local private_dir_uid
    local process_uid
    test "$private_active" = 1 || return 1
    if preseal_harness_active; then
        case "$private_dir" in
            /private/tmp/task-4.2-r04-core-matrix.??????/private-preseal.??????) ;;
            *) return 1 ;;
        esac
    else
        case "$private_dir" in
            /private/tmp/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal.??????) ;;
            *) return 1 ;;
        esac
    fi
    test -d "$private_dir" || return 1
    test ! -L "$private_dir" || return 1
    canonical_private_dir="$(cd "$private_dir" && pwd -P)" || return 1
    test "$canonical_private_dir" = "$private_dir" || return 1
    private_dir_uid="$(stat -f '%u' "$private_dir")" || return 1
    process_uid="$(id -u)" || return 1
    test "$private_dir_uid" = "$process_uid" || return 1
    private_dir_mode="$(stat -f '%Lp' "$private_dir")" || return 1
    test "$private_dir_mode" = 700 || return 1
}

require_private_regular() {
    local private_mode
    local private_path
    private_path="$1"
    private_mode="$2"
    authenticate_private_dir || return 1
    test "${private_path%/*}" = "$private_dir" || return 1
    case "$private_path" in
        "$private_diagnostic"|"$private_output"|"$private_status") ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    require_owned_regular_mode "$private_path" "$private_mode" || return 1
}

require_not_alias() {
    local alias_status
    if test "$1" -ef "$2"; then
        return 1
    else
        alias_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$alias_status" = 1 || return 1
}

authenticate_private_paths() {
    require_private_regular "$private_diagnostic" 600 || return 1
    require_private_regular "$private_output" 600 || return 1
    require_private_regular "$private_status" 600 || return 1
    require_not_alias "$private_diagnostic" "$private_output" || return 1
    require_not_alias "$private_diagnostic" "$private_status" || return 1
    require_not_alias "$private_output" "$private_status" || return 1
}

authenticate_private_residual_inventory() {
    local private_entry
    authenticate_private_dir || return 1
    for private_entry in \
        "$private_dir"/* "$private_dir"/.[!.]* "$private_dir"/..?*; do
        if [ ! -e "$private_entry" ] && [ ! -L "$private_entry" ]; then
            continue
        fi
        case "$private_entry" in
            "$private_diagnostic"|"$private_output"|"$private_status") ;;
            *) return 1 ;;
        esac
        require_private_regular "$private_entry" 600 || return 1
    done
}

authenticate_private_exact_inventory() {
    test "$private_bundle_complete" = 1 || return 1
    authenticate_private_paths || return 1
    authenticate_private_residual_inventory || return 1
}

classify_private_residual() {
    private_residual_class='setup-residual'
    private_residual_auth='FAIL'
    if authenticate_private_residual_inventory; then
        private_residual_auth='PASS'
    fi
    if [ "$private_cleanup_committed" = 1 ]; then
        private_residual_class='cleanup-residual'
    elif [ "$private_bundle_complete" = 1 ] &&
         authenticate_private_exact_inventory; then
        private_residual_class='exact-bundle'
        private_residual_auth='PASS'
    fi
}

authenticate_private_fd_bindings() {
    test "$private_diag_fd_open" = 1 || return 1
    test "$private_output_fd_open" = 1 || return 1
    test "$private_status_fd_open" = 1 || return 1
    authenticate_private_paths || return 1
    require_private_fd_binding 3 "$private_diagnostic" || return 1
    require_private_fd_binding 4 "$private_output" || return 1
    require_private_fd_binding 5 "$private_status" || return 1
}

require_private_fd_binding() {
    local fd_inode
    local fd_link_count
    local fd_number
    local fd_size
    local fd_uid
    local private_inode
    local private_link_count
    local private_path
    local private_size
    local private_uid
    fd_number="$1"
    private_path="$2"
    case "$fd_number:$private_path" in
        "3:$private_diagnostic"|"4:$private_output"|"5:$private_status") ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
    test -w "/dev/fd/$fd_number" || return 1
    fd_inode="$(stat -f '%i' "/dev/fd/$fd_number")" || return 1
    private_inode="$(stat -f '%i' "$private_path")" || return 1
    test "$fd_inode" = "$private_inode" || return 1
    fd_uid="$(stat -f '%u' "/dev/fd/$fd_number")" || return 1
    private_uid="$(stat -f '%u' "$private_path")" || return 1
    test "$fd_uid" = "$private_uid" || return 1
    fd_size="$(stat -f '%z' "/dev/fd/$fd_number")" || return 1
    private_size="$(stat -f '%z' "$private_path")" || return 1
    test "$fd_size" = "$private_size" || return 1
    fd_link_count="$(stat -f '%l' "/dev/fd/$fd_number")" || return 1
    private_link_count="$(stat -f '%l' "$private_path")" || return 1
    test "$fd_link_count" = "$private_link_count" || return 1
}

preseal_log() {
    test "$private_diag_fd_open" = 1 || return 1
    printf '%s\n' "$1" >&3 || return 1
}

preseal_enter_stage() {
    local diagnostic_status
    local stage_code
    preseal_stage="$1"
    stage_code="$2"
    preseal_predicate='none'
    if preseal_log "stage=$preseal_stage"; then
        diagnostic_status=0
    else
        diagnostic_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$diagnostic_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_predicate='diagnostic-write'
        return "$stage_code"
    fi
}

preseal_record_failure() {
    local diagnostic_status
    local stage_code
    stage_code="$1"
    preseal_predicate="$2"
    diagnostic_status=0
    if [ "$private_diag_fd_open" = 1 ]; then
        if printf '%s\n' \
            "failure-stage=$preseal_stage|predicate=$preseal_predicate|stage-code=$stage_code|helper-status=$preseal_helper_status" >&3; then
            diagnostic_status=0
        else
            diagnostic_status="$?"
        fi
    fi
    if [ "$diagnostic_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_predicate='diagnostic-write'
    fi
    return "$stage_code"
}

preseal_require() {
    local guard_status
    local predicate
    local stage_code
    stage_code="$1"
    predicate="$2"
    shift 2
    if "$@"; then
        guard_status=0
    else
        guard_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$guard_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure "$stage_code" "$predicate"
        return "$?"
    fi
}

close_private_output_status_fds() {
    local close_status
    local combined_status
    combined_status=0
    if [ "$private_output_fd_open" = 1 ]; then
        if exec 4>&-; then
            private_output_fd_open=0
        else
            close_status="$?"
            combined_status=1
        fi
    fi
    if [ "$private_status_fd_open" = 1 ]; then
        if exec 5>&-; then
            private_status_fd_open=0
        else
            close_status="$?"
            combined_status=1
        fi
    fi
    return "$combined_status"
}

preserve_private_on_exit() {
    local original_status="$?"
    local private_residual_auth
    local private_residual_class
    trap - EXIT
    trap '' HUP INT TERM
    if [ "$private_active" != 1 ]; then
        exit "$original_status"
    fi
    if [ "$original_status" = 0 ]; then
        original_status=80
        preseal_predicate='unexpected-zero-exit-with-private-active'
    fi
    set +e
    classify_private_residual
    if [ "$private_diag_fd_open" = 1 ]; then
        printf '%s\n' \
            "exit-stage=$preseal_stage|predicate=$preseal_predicate|exit=$original_status|helper-status=$preseal_helper_status|private-state=$private_residual_class|residual-auth=$private_residual_auth|bundle-complete=$private_bundle_complete|cleanup-committed=$private_cleanup_committed" >&3
    fi
    if [ "$private_output_fd_open" = 1 ]; then exec 4>&-; fi
    if [ "$private_status_fd_open" = 1 ]; then exec 5>&-; fi
    if [ "$private_diag_fd_open" = 1 ]; then exec 3>&-; fi
    private_output_fd_open=0
    private_status_fd_open=0
    private_diag_fd_open=0
    printf '%s\n' \
        "Task 4.2 correction r04 private preseal residual: path=$private_dir|private-state=$private_residual_class|residual-auth=$private_residual_auth|bundle-complete=$private_bundle_complete|cleanup-committed=$private_cleanup_committed|stage=$preseal_stage|predicate=$preseal_predicate|exit=$original_status|helper-status=$preseal_helper_status" >&2
    exit "$original_status"
}

preseal_signal() {
    local signal_code
    local signal_name
    signal_name="$1"
    signal_code="$2"
    preseal_predicate="signal-$signal_name"
    exit "$signal_code"
}

open_private_fds() {
    local diagnostic_open_status
    local noclobber_restore_status
    local output_open_status
    local private_open_umask
    local status_open_status
    local umask_restore_status
    require_capture_fds_closed || return 80
    require_absent "$private_diagnostic" || return 80
    require_absent "$private_output" || return 80
    require_absent "$private_status" || return 80
    private_open_umask="$(umask)" || return 80
    diagnostic_open_status=1
    output_open_status=1
    status_open_status=1
    if umask 077; then
        :
    else
        return 80
    fi
    if set -o noclobber; then
        :
    else
        umask "$private_open_umask" || :
        return 80
    fi
    if exec 3> "$private_diagnostic"; then
        diagnostic_open_status=0
        private_diag_fd_open=1
        if exec 4> "$private_output"; then
            output_open_status=0
        else
            output_open_status="$?"
        fi
    else
        diagnostic_open_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$output_open_status" = 0 ]; then
        private_output_fd_open=1
        if exec 5> "$private_status"; then
            status_open_status=0
        else
            status_open_status="$?"
        fi
    fi
    if [ "$status_open_status" = 0 ]; then
        private_status_fd_open=1
    fi
    if set +o noclobber; then
        noclobber_restore_status=0
    else
        noclobber_restore_status="$?"
    fi
    if umask "$private_open_umask"; then
        umask_restore_status=0
    else
        umask_restore_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$diagnostic_open_status" = 0 || return 80
    test "$output_open_status" = 0 || return 80
    test "$status_open_status" = 0 || return 80
    test "$noclobber_restore_status" = 0 || return 80
    test "$umask_restore_status" = 0 || return 80
    authenticate_private_fd_bindings || return 80
}

authenticate_private_cleanup_preflight() {
    test "$private_active" = 1 || return 1
    test "$private_bundle_complete" = 1 || return 1
    test "$private_cleanup_committed" = 0 || return 1
    test "$private_diag_fd_open" = 1 || return 1
    test "$private_output_fd_open" = 0 || return 1
    test "$private_status_fd_open" = 0 || return 1
    authenticate_private_exact_inventory || return 1
    require_private_fd_binding 3 "$private_diagnostic" || return 1
}

finish_private_success() {
    local close_status
    preseal_enter_stage cleanup 126 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 126 'cleanup-private-authentication' \
        authenticate_private_cleanup_preflight || return "$?"
    preseal_inject_or_fail 126 cleanup-precommit || return "$?"
    preseal_log \
        'cleanup-commit=authorized|private-inventory=exact-three|destruction=output,status,diagnostic,directory' || \
        { preseal_record_failure 126 'cleanup-commit-log'; return "$?"; }
    trap '' HUP INT TERM
    if exec 3>&-; then
        close_status=0
    else
        close_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$close_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 126 'cleanup-diagnostic-fd-close'
        return "$?"
    fi
    private_diag_fd_open=0
    private_cleanup_committed=1
    /bin/rm "$private_output" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 126 'cleanup-remove-output'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_inject_or_fail 126 cleanup-after-first-unlink || return "$?"
    /bin/rm "$private_status" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 126 'cleanup-remove-status'; return "$?"; }
    /bin/rm "$private_diagnostic" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 126 'cleanup-remove-diagnostic'; return "$?"; }
    /bin/rmdir "$private_dir" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 126 'cleanup-remove-directory'; return "$?"; }
    trap - EXIT HUP INT TERM
    private_active=0
    private_bundle_complete=0
    private_cleanup_committed=0
    private_diagnostic=''
    private_output=''
    private_status=''
    private_dir=''
}

capture_preseal() {
    local close_status
    local helper_status_write_status
    local output_write_status
    local publication_noclobber_restore_status
    local publication_noclobber_set_status
    local postpublish_validation_status
    local private_fd_open_status
    local private_template
    local private_validation_status
    local publication_umask
    local publication_umask_set_status
    local restore_status
    local status_write_status
    local suite_umask_observed
    preseal_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt"
    preseal_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt"
    private_bundle_complete=0
    private_cleanup_committed=0
    case "$-" in *e*|*C*) return 80 ;; esac
    require_capture_fds_closed || return 80
    require_absent "$preseal_output" || return 80
    require_absent "$preseal_status" || return 80

    private_template="$(preseal_private_template)" || return 80
    private_dir="$(/usr/bin/mktemp -d "$private_template")" || return 80
    private_diagnostic="$private_dir/diagnostic.txt"
    private_output="$private_dir/output.txt"
    private_status="$private_dir/status.txt"
    private_active=1
    trap preserve_private_on_exit EXIT
    trap 'preseal_signal HUP 129' HUP
    trap 'preseal_signal INT 130' INT
    trap 'preseal_signal TERM 143' TERM

    preseal_inject_or_fail 80 setup-after-mkdir || return "$?"
    authenticate_private_dir || \
        { preseal_predicate='private-directory-authentication'; return 80; }
    if open_private_fds; then
        private_fd_open_status=0
    else
        private_fd_open_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$private_fd_open_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 80 'private-fixed-fd-open'
        return "$?"
    fi
    private_bundle_complete=1
    authenticate_private_exact_inventory || \
        { private_bundle_complete=0; preseal_record_failure 80 'private-bundle-completion'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_log 'schema=task-4.2-correction-r04-private-preseal-v1' || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'diagnostic-schema-write'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_log "plan-anchor=$plan_anchor" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'diagnostic-anchor-write'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_log "helper-sha256=$helper_sha" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'diagnostic-helper-write'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_log 'private-create-umask=077' || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'diagnostic-private-umask-write'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_log 'suite-child-umask=022' || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'diagnostic-suite-umask-write'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_log 'private-bundle-complete=1' || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'diagnostic-bundle-complete-write'; return "$?"; }
    preseal_inject_or_fail 80 after-bundle || return "$?"

    preseal_enter_stage preflight 80 || return "$?"
    preseal_inject_or_fail 80 preflight || return "$?"
    preseal_require 80 'preflight-common' require_common || return "$?"
    preseal_require 80 'preflight-ledgers-110' verify_ledgers 110 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 80 'preflight-manifest-53' verify_manifest 53 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 80 'preflight-round-baseline' \
        require_round_baseline || return "$?"
    preseal_require 80 'preflight-output-absent' \
        require_absent "$preseal_output" || return "$?"
    preseal_require 80 'preflight-status-absent' \
        require_absent "$preseal_status" || return "$?"
    suite_umask_observed="$(umask 022; umask)" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'suite-umask-probe'; return "$?"; }
    test "$suite_umask_observed" = 0022 || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'suite-umask-not-0022'; return "$?"; }
    printf '%s\n' "$row_prefix preseal" >&4 || \
        { preseal_record_failure 80 'private-marker-write'; return "$?"; }

    preseal_enter_stage helper 81 || return "$?"
    set +e
    (
        set -euo pipefail
        set +o braceexpand
        umask 022
        exec 3>&-
        exec 5>&-
        /bin/bash "$suite_path"
    ) >&4 2>&1
    preseal_helper_status="$?"
    printf '%s\n' "$preseal_helper_status" >&5
    helper_status_write_status="$?"
    close_private_output_status_fds
    close_status="$?"
    if [ "$helper_status_write_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 82 'private-status-write'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$close_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 82 'private-output-status-fd-close'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$preseal_helper_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 81 'helper-nonzero'
        return "$?"
    fi
    preseal_inject_or_fail 82 after-helper || return "$?"

    preseal_enter_stage private-validation 82 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 82 'private-path-authentication' \
        authenticate_private_paths || return "$?"
    require_private_fd_binding 3 "$private_diagnostic" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 82 'private-diagnostic-fd-binding'; return "$?"; }
    if validate_preseal_pair_at "$private_output" "$private_status"; then
        private_validation_status=0
    else
        private_validation_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$private_validation_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 82 "$preseal_predicate"
        return "$?"
    fi

    preseal_enter_stage prepublication 83 || return "$?"
    preseal_inject_or_fail 83 prepublication || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-private-authentication' \
        authenticate_private_paths || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-common' require_common || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-ledgers-110' verify_ledgers 110 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-manifest-53' verify_manifest 53 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-round-baseline' \
        require_round_baseline || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-output-absent' \
        require_absent "$preseal_output" || return "$?"
    preseal_require 83 'prepublication-status-absent' \
        require_absent "$preseal_status" || return "$?"

    preseal_enter_stage output-publication 84 || return "$?"
    publication_umask="$(umask)" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 84 'output-publication-read-umask'; return "$?"; }
    if umask 077; then
        publication_umask_set_status=0
    else
        publication_umask_set_status="$?"
    fi
    if set -o noclobber; then
        publication_noclobber_set_status=0
    else
        publication_noclobber_set_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_umask_set_status" = 0 ] &&
       [ "$publication_noclobber_set_status" = 0 ]; then
        preseal_inject_or_fail 84 output-before-copy || return "$?"
        if /bin/cat "$private_output" > "$preseal_output"; then
            output_write_status=0
        else
            output_write_status="$?"
        fi
    else
        output_write_status=NOT-RUN
    fi
    if set +o noclobber; then
        publication_noclobber_restore_status=0
    else
        publication_noclobber_restore_status="$?"
    fi
    if umask "$publication_umask"; then
        restore_status=0
    else
        restore_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_umask_set_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 84 'output-publication-set-umask'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_noclobber_set_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 84 'output-publication-set-noclobber'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$output_write_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 84 'output-publication-write'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_noclobber_restore_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 84 'output-publication-noclobber-restore'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$restore_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 84 'output-publication-umask-restore'
        return "$?"
    fi
    preseal_inject_or_fail 84 after-output || return "$?"

    preseal_enter_stage status-publication 85 || return "$?"
    publication_umask="$(umask)" || \
        { preseal_record_failure 85 'status-publication-read-umask'; return "$?"; }
    if umask 077; then
        publication_umask_set_status=0
    else
        publication_umask_set_status="$?"
    fi
    if set -o noclobber; then
        publication_noclobber_set_status=0
    else
        publication_noclobber_set_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_umask_set_status" = 0 ] &&
       [ "$publication_noclobber_set_status" = 0 ]; then
        preseal_inject_or_fail 85 status-before-copy || return "$?"
        if /bin/cat "$private_status" > "$preseal_status"; then
            status_write_status=0
        else
            status_write_status="$?"
        fi
    else
        status_write_status=NOT-RUN
    fi
    if set +o noclobber; then
        publication_noclobber_restore_status=0
    else
        publication_noclobber_restore_status="$?"
    fi
    if umask "$publication_umask"; then
        restore_status=0
    else
        restore_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_umask_set_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 85 'status-publication-set-umask'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_noclobber_set_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 85 'status-publication-set-noclobber'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$status_write_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 85 'status-publication-write'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$publication_noclobber_restore_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 85 'status-publication-noclobber-restore'
        return "$?"
    fi
    if [ "$restore_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 85 'status-publication-umask-restore'
        return "$?"
    fi

    preseal_enter_stage postpublication 86 || return "$?"
    preseal_inject_or_fail 86 postpublication-before-validation || return "$?"
    if validate_preseal_pair_at "$preseal_output" "$preseal_status"; then
        postpublish_validation_status=0
    else
        postpublish_validation_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$postpublish_validation_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 86 "$preseal_predicate"
        return "$?"
    fi
    preseal_require 86 'postpublication-output-bytes' \
        cmp -s "$private_output" "$preseal_output" || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'postpublication-status-bytes' \
        cmp -s "$private_status" "$preseal_status" || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'postpublication-manifest-54' verify_manifest 54 || return "$?"
    return 0
}

mode_preseal() {
    local capture_status
    local cleanup_status
    local generated_status
    local preseal_record
    local suite_record
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    verify_ledgers 110 || return 1
    verify_manifest 53 || return 1
    capture_preseal
    capture_status="$?"
    if [ "$capture_status" != 0 ]; then
        return "$capture_status"
    fi
    preseal_enter_stage ledger-closure 86 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-public-pair' validate_preseal_pair || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-manifest-54-before-rows' verify_manifest 54 || return "$?"
    if preseal_record="$(preseal_value)"; then
        generated_status=0
    else
        generated_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$generated_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 86 'closure-preseal-value'
        return "$?"
    fi
    preseal_inject_or_fail 86 closure-before-row111 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-row-111' \
        append_row_pair 110 'preseal evidence' "$preseal_record" || return "$?"
    if suite_record="$(suite_value)"; then
        generated_status=0
    else
        generated_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$generated_status" != 0 ]; then
        preseal_record_failure 86 'closure-suite-value'
        return "$?"
    fi
    preseal_require 86 'closure-row-112' \
        append_row_pair 111 'preseal full suite' "$suite_record" || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-common' require_common || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-ledgers-112' verify_ledgers 112 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-manifest-54' verify_manifest 54 || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-helper-row' require_helper_row || return "$?"
    preseal_require 86 'closure-round-baseline' require_round_baseline || return "$?"
    if finish_private_success; then
        cleanup_status=0
    else
        cleanup_status="$?"
    fi
    if [ "$cleanup_status" != 0 ]; then
        return "$cleanup_status"
    fi
}

task42_preseal_dispatch() {
    local mode_status
    local restore_errexit
    restore_errexit=0
    case "$-" in *e*) restore_errexit=1 ;; esac
    set +e
    mode_preseal "$@"
    mode_status="$?"
    if [ "$restore_errexit" = 1 ]; then
        set -e
    fi
    return "$mode_status"
}
# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_END

emit_expected_seal() {
    local progress_bytes
    local progress_sha
    local report_bytes
    local report_sha
    verify_ledgers 112 || return 1
    verify_manifest 54 || return 1
    require_helper_row || return 1
    printf '%s\n' '# Task 4.2 Review-Correction Evidence Seal' || return 1
    printf '%s\n' \
        'Evidence seal schema: task-4.2-review-correction-v3' || return 1
    printf '%s\n' 'Accepted correction round: r04' || return 1
    printf '%s\n' "Rejected correction round: r01|tip=$r01_anchor|parent=$design_commit|subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r01|scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path|driver-sha256=$r01_driver_sha|failure-stage=task-2-baseline|exit=2|diagnostic=test-too-many-arguments|root-cause=apple-bash-3.2-brace-expansion|test-commit=ABSENT|review-returns=NONE|review-rows=0|ledger-suffix-bytes=9842|ledger-suffix-sha256=16836a3f3ae986761d8eee5bd77fe26e2336162c490e26479dfa6cda3c43503c|manifest-materialized=26|manifest-reserved=108|seal=ABSENT|ledger-rows=44|artifacts=26" || return 1
    printf '%s\n' "Rejected correction round: r02|plan-anchor=$r02_plan_anchor|plan-parent=$r01_anchor|plan-subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r02|plan-scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path|tip=$r02_tip|tip-parent=$r02_plan_anchor|tip-subject=test: correct Task 4.2 behavioral evidence r02|tip-scope=$initial_tests_path,$support_path|driver-sha256=$r02_driver_sha|failure-stage=task-6-shell-gate|diagnostic=canonical-preseal-created-before-manifest-53|review-return-specification=identity=/root/task_4_2_r02_specification_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-return-behavioral-mutation=identity=/root/task_4_2_r02_behavioral_mutation_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-return-shell-gate=identity=/root/task_4_2_r02_shell_gate_review;verdict=REJECT;critical=1;important=0;minor=1|review-return-compatibility=identity=/root/task_4_2_r02_compatibility_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-rows=0|controller-artifacts=0|preseal-artifacts=0|ledger-suffix-bytes=30310|ledger-suffix-sha256=66c8ee9925972f0ff7059db0d053d6e150539cc4f2910cbb3d337820750013e9|manifest-materialized=82|manifest-reserved=52|seal=ABSENT|ledger-rows=80|artifacts=82" || return 1
    printf '%s\n' "Rejected correction round: r03|plan-anchor=$r03_plan_anchor|plan-parent=$r02_tip|plan-subject=docs: anchor Task 4.2 correction r03|plan-scope=$legacy_plan_path,$plan_path|tip=$r03_plan_anchor|driver-sha256=$r03_driver_sha|failure-stage=task-8-preseal|exit=1|diagnostic=helper-umask-077-descriptor-mode-no-op|root-cause=private-umask-leaked-into-helper|review-return-specification=identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_specification_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-return-behavioral-mutation=identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_behavioral_mutation_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-return-shell-gate=identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_shell_gate_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-return-compatibility=identity=/root/task_4_2_r03_compatibility_review;verdict=APPROVE;critical=0;important=0|review-rows=4|controller-artifacts=50|preseal-output=ABSENT|preseal-status=ABSENT|private-preseal=REMOVED-BY-REJECTED-TRAP|ledger-suffix-bytes=$r03_suffix_bytes|ledger-suffix-sha256=$r03_suffix_sha|manifest-materialized=132|manifest-reserved=2|seal=ABSENT|ledger-rows=110|artifacts=132" || return 1
    emit_expected_suffix 112 || return 1
    report_bytes="$(file_bytes "$report_path")" || return 1
    report_sha="$(sha256_file "$report_path")" || return 1
    progress_bytes="$(file_bytes "$progress_path")" || return 1
    progress_sha="$(sha256_file "$progress_path")" || return 1
    printf '%s\n' \
        "Frozen report ledger: path=$report_path|bytes=$report_bytes|sha256=$report_sha" || return 1
    printf '%s\n' \
        "Frozen progress ledger: path=$progress_path|bytes=$progress_bytes|sha256=$progress_sha" || return 1
}

require_closure() {
    local seal_generation_status
    local seal_line_count
    require_common || return 1
    require_round_baseline || return 1
    verify_ledgers 112 || return 1
    verify_manifest 54 || return 1
    require_helper_row || return 1
    if seal_line_count="$(emit_expected_seal |
        wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')"; then
        seal_generation_status=0
    else
        seal_generation_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$seal_generation_status" = 0 || return 1
    test "$seal_line_count" = 120 || return 1
}

mode_emit_seal() {
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_closure || return 1
    emit_expected_seal || return 1
}

require_unstaged_seal_candidate() {
    local seal_tracked_status
    require_exact_output "$plan_anchor" git rev-parse HEAD || return 1
    require_corrected_tests || return 1
    require_production_hashes || return 1
    require_empty_index || return 1
    require_exact_output '' git diff --name-only || return 1
    require_exact_output "$seal_path" \
        git ls-files --others --exclude-standard || return 1
    test -f "$seal_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$seal_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 120 file_lines "$seal_path" || return 1
    if git ls-files --error-unmatch -- "$seal_path" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        return 1
    else
        seal_tracked_status="$?"
    fi
    test "$seal_tracked_status" = 1 || return 1
    emit_expected_seal | cmp -s - "$seal_path" || return 1
}

mode_verify_seal() {
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    verify_ledgers 112 || return 1
    verify_manifest 54 || return 1
    require_helper_row || return 1
    require_unstaged_seal_candidate || return 1
}

require_staged_seal_candidate() {
    require_exact_output "$plan_anchor" git rev-parse HEAD || return 1
    require_corrected_tests || return 1
    require_exact_output "$seal_path" \
        git diff --cached --name-only || return 1
    require_exact_output '' git diff --name-only || return 1
    require_no_nonignored_untracked || return 1
    test -f "$seal_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$seal_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 120 file_lines "$seal_path" || return 1
    emit_expected_seal | cmp -s - "$seal_path" || return 1
    git show ":$seal_path" | cmp -s - "$seal_path" || return 1
    git diff --cached --check || return 1
}

mode_pre_seal_commit() {
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    verify_ledgers 112 || return 1
    verify_manifest 54 || return 1
    require_helper_row || return 1
    require_staged_seal_candidate || return 1
}

verify_terminal_static() {
    require_exact_output "$seal_commit" git rev-parse HEAD || return 1
    require_fixed_history || return 1
    require_seal_commit "$seal_commit" || return 1
    require_git_clean || return 1
    require_common || return 1
    require_corrected_tests || return 1
    verify_ledgers 112 || return 1
    verify_manifest 54 || return 1
    require_helper_row || return 1
    test -f "$seal_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$seal_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 120 file_lines "$seal_path" || return 1
    git show "$seal_commit:$seal_path" | cmp -s - "$seal_path" || return 1
    emit_expected_seal | cmp -s - "$seal_path" || return 1
}

mode_terminal() {
    test "$#" = 0 || return 1
    seal_commit="$(git rev-parse HEAD)" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$plan_anchor" git rev-parse HEAD^ || return 1
    verify_terminal_static || return 1
    require_helper_row || return 1
    run_full_suite || return 1
    verify_terminal_static || return 1
    require_git_clean || return 1
}

case "$mode" in
    anchor) mode_anchor "$@" || return 1 ;;
    baseline) mode_baseline "$@" || return 1 ;;
    red) mode_red "$@" || return 1 ;;
    implementer) mode_implementer "$@" || return 1 ;;
    gate-suites) mode_gate_suites "$@" || return 1 ;;
    record-review) mode_record_review "$@" || return 1 ;;
    controller) mode_controller "$@" || return 1 ;;
    preseal) task42_preseal_dispatch "$@" ;;
    emit-seal) mode_emit_seal "$@" || return 1 ;;
    verify-seal) mode_verify_seal "$@" || return 1 ;;
    pre-seal-commit) mode_pre_seal_commit "$@" || return 1 ;;
    terminal) mode_terminal "$@" || return 1 ;;
    *) exit 64 ;;
esac
}
TASK42_DRIVER_READY=task-4.2-correction-r04-driver-complete-v1
# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_END
```

The task-local launchers below repeat the complete fail-closed extraction
sequence. The buffered extractor emits no driver bytes until it has observed
exactly one ordered marker pair and a nonempty body. The only invocation of
`task42_main` is appended after successful extraction. `pipefail` therefore
propagates an empty, truncated, failed, or signal-terminated producer, even if
the downstream shell could parse its prefix. Each launcher authenticates the
definition-only body, syntax-checks the exact combined stream, reauthenticates
the body immediately before execution, strips shell startup variables, and
gives the running driver `/dev/null` rather than its source stream as stdin.

This is the exact Task 1 launcher. Later tasks repeat it literally, changing
only the documented `HEAD` selector and final `run_driver` call arguments.

```bash
/usr/bin/env -i \
  TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
  PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
  LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
  /bin/bash --noprofile --norc <<'TASK42_LAUNCHER'
set -euo pipefail
PLAN_PATH='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
DRIVER_SHA='247f7a9f6c9979a0edbc8b5236f99ab584d2775ce6c437d443c72423ebcd1667'
P="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git rev-parse HEAD)" || exit 1
/bin/test "${#P}" = 40 || exit 1
case "$P" in *[!0-9a-f]*) exit 64 ;; esac
verified_p="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git \
  rev-parse --verify "$P^{commit}")" || exit 1
/bin/test "$verified_p" = "$P" || exit 1

extract_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git --no-pager show "$p:$PLAN_PATH" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk \
      -v begin='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
      -v end='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_END' '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
  bc++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {
  ec++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
  if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}

driver_body_sha() {
  extract_driver "$1" |
    /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk '
{
  n++
  if (n != 1 || NF != 2 || $2 != "-" || length($1) != 64 ||
      $1 ~ /[^0-9a-f]/) bad=1
  value=$1
}
END {
  if (bad || n != 1) exit 74
  print value
}'
}

emit_runnable_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  local rc
  if extract_driver "$p"; then
    /usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
      '[ "${TASK42_DRIVER_READY-}" = task-4.2-correction-r04-driver-complete-v1 ] || exit 125' \
      'task42_main_type="$(builtin type -t task42_main)" || exit 125' \
      '[ "$task42_main_type" = function ] || exit 125' \
      'task42_main "$@" </dev/null'
  else
    rc=$?
    return "$rc"
  fi
}

run_driver() {
  local actual_driver_sha
  local driver_status
  local p
  /bin/test "$#" -ge 2 || return 1
  p="$2"
  actual_driver_sha="$(driver_body_sha "$p")" || return 1
  /bin/test "$actual_driver_sha" = "$DRIVER_SHA" || return 1
  emit_runnable_driver "$p" |
    /usr/bin/env -i \
      TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
      PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
      LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -n -s -- "$@" || return 1
  actual_driver_sha="$(driver_body_sha "$p")" || return 1
  /bin/test "$actual_driver_sha" = "$DRIVER_SHA" || return 1
  set +e
  emit_runnable_driver "$p" |
    /usr/bin/env -i \
      TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
      PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
      LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -s -- "$@"
  driver_status="$?"
  set -e
  return "$driver_status"
}

run_driver anchor "$P" || exit 1
run_driver anchor "$P" || exit 1
TASK42_LAUNCHER
```

## Controller-Only Pre-Dispatch Bootstrap

This bootstrap is the only state-changing work before Task 1, and the root
controller performs it serially. Do not delegate it. It proves exact clean P3,
the complete physical ledger prefixes, frozen `r01`, `r02`, and `r03` namespaces, the
fresh `r04` 26/108 namespace, exact two-plan working scope, and the embedded
driver before creating P4. No `r04` row may be appended until P4 exists and has
been reverified.

- [ ] **Step 1: Verify the uncommitted P4 candidate without changing Git,
  either ledger, or any evidence artifact.**

```bash
/usr/bin/env -i \
  TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
  PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
  LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
  /bin/bash --noprofile --norc <<'TASK42_BOOTSTRAP_VERIFY'
set -euo pipefail
set +o braceexpand
export GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1
cd /Users/jlsor/Documents/Research/SSR/.worktrees/ssr-oracle-passive-trace

BASE='98020fe367ab8dd922e2a2703502c0d8edb0db6a'
T2='489cc1ce2a06e5a6db60759f5484ed2f170b4d5a'
P2='2471b92048592f13928969c04395f3e9495a476c'
R01='360436f8667407f5360fdf54701ab999a2729552'
DESIGN='b2eb61d0ab046fe613941fd60d9e28496db8511a'
IMPLEMENTATION='622abf4d22232ecdf3dfa831d02a985578a145f3'
LEGACY_PLAN='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-31-oracle-passive-plugin.md'
PLAN_PATH='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
ARTIFACT_DIR='.superpowers/sdd/2026-07-31-oracle-passive-plugin'
REPORT_PATH="$ARTIFACT_DIR/task-4.2-report.md"
PROGRESS_PATH="$ARTIFACT_DIR/progress.md"
DRIVER_SHA='247f7a9f6c9979a0edbc8b5236f99ab584d2775ce6c437d443c72423ebcd1667'

file_sha() {
  /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 -- "$1" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk 'NR == 1 && NF == 2 && length($1) == 64 &&
      $1 !~ /[^0-9a-f]/ { print $1; ok=1 } END { if (!ok || NR != 1) exit 74 }'
}

stream_sha() {
  /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk 'NR == 1 && NF == 2 && $2 == "-" &&
      length($1) == 64 && $1 !~ /[^0-9a-f]/ {
        print $1; ok=1
      } END { if (!ok || NR != 1) exit 74 }'
}

require_exact_output() {
  local expected_output="$1"
  local actual_output
  shift
  actual_output="$("$@")" || return 1
  test "$actual_output" = "$expected_output" || return 1
}

file_bytes() {
  /usr/bin/wc -c < "$1" | /usr/bin/tr -d '[:space:]'
}

file_lines() {
  /usr/bin/wc -l < "$1" | /usr/bin/tr -d '[:space:]'
}

slice_base64() {
  /bin/dd if="$1" bs=1 skip="$2" count="$3" 2>/dev/null |
    /usr/bin/base64
}

slice_sha() {
  /bin/dd if="$1" bs=1 skip="$2" count="$3" 2>/dev/null |
    stream_sha
}

blob_sha() {
  /usr/bin/git --no-pager show "$1:$2" | stream_sha
}

commit_scope() {
  /usr/bin/git diff-tree --no-commit-id --name-only -r "$1" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/sort
}

driver_blob_sha() {
  extract_driver_blob "$1" "$2" "$3" | stream_sha
}

driver_file_sha() {
  extract_driver_file "$1" "$2" "$3" | stream_sha
}

sorted_worktree_diff() {
  /usr/bin/git diff --name-only | LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/sort
}

extract_driver_file() {
  local source_path="$1"
  local begin="$2"
  local end="$3"
  LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk -v begin="$begin" -v end="$end" '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
  bc++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {
  ec++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
  if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}' "$source_path"
}

extract_driver_blob() {
  local commit="$1"
  local begin="$2"
  local end="$3"
  GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git --no-pager show \
    "$commit:$PLAN_PATH" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk -v begin="$begin" -v end="$end" '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
  bc++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {
  ec++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
  if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}

manifest_paths() {
  local r="$1"
  local mutation_ids
  printf '%s/task-4.2-review-correction-%s-brief.md\n' \
    "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" || return 1
  mutation_ids="$(/usr/bin/jot -w '%02d' 25 1)" || return 1
  for n in $mutation_ids; do
    printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-M%s.patch\n' \
      "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" "$n" || return 1
  done
  for n in 03 07 10; do
    printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-red-M%s-output.txt\n' \
      "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" "$n" || return 1
    printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-red-M%s-status.txt\n' \
      "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" "$n" || return 1
  done
  for role in implementer controller; do
    for n in $mutation_ids; do
      printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-%s-M%s-output.txt\n' \
        "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" "$role" "$n" || return 1
      printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-%s-M%s-status.txt\n' \
        "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" "$role" "$n" || return 1
    done
  done
  printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-preseal-output.txt\n' \
    "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" || return 1
  printf '%s/task-4.2-correction-%s-preseal-status.txt\n' \
    "$ARTIFACT_DIR" "$r" || return 1
}

actual_round_paths() {
  local r="$1"
  local candidate_name
  for candidate in "$ARTIFACT_DIR"/*; do
    if [ ! -e "$candidate" ] && [ ! -L "$candidate" ]; then continue; fi
    candidate_name="$(basename "$candidate")" || return 1
    case "$candidate_name" in
      task-4.2-correction-$r-*|task-4.2-review-correction-$r-brief.md)
        printf '%s\n' "$candidate" || return 1
        ;;
    esac
  done | LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/sort
}

verify_namespace() {
  local r="$1"
  local materialized="$2"
  local actual_manifest
  local expected_manifest
  local manifest_count
  local materialized_manifest
  local ordinal
  expected_manifest="$(manifest_paths "$r")" || return 1
  manifest_count="$(printf '%s\n' "$expected_manifest" |
    /usr/bin/wc -l | /usr/bin/tr -d '[:space:]')" || return 1
  test "$manifest_count" = 134 || return 1
  ordinal=0
  while IFS= read -r artifact_path; do
    ordinal=$((ordinal + 1))
    if [ "$ordinal" -le "$materialized" ]; then
      test -f "$artifact_path" || return 1
      test ! -L "$artifact_path" || return 1
    else
      test ! -e "$artifact_path" || return 1
      test ! -L "$artifact_path" || return 1
    fi
  done <<< "$expected_manifest"
  test "$ordinal" = 134 || return 1
  actual_manifest="$(actual_round_paths "$r")" || return 1
  materialized_manifest="$(printf '%s\n' "$expected_manifest" |
    /usr/bin/sed -n "1,${materialized}p" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/sort)" || return 1
  test "$actual_manifest" = "$materialized_manifest" || return 1
}

r02_mutation_meta() {
  local mutation_id="$1"
  case "$mutation_id" in
    01) registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='any two captures do not settle' ;;
    02) registration='pre epoch neutral does not leak'; fragment='new epoch neutral enables pair' ;;
    03) registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement requires two fresh candidate captures' ;;
    04) registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='nonquiescent sample cleared candidate' ;;
    05) registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='cardinal input cleared candidate' ;;
    06) registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement neutral does not leak to next callback' ;;
    07) registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement callback is frame one' ;;
    08) registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='new epoch pair settles inside reset deadline' ;;
    09) registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement cleared old last_capture' ;;
    10) registration='replacement rebases same callback'; fragment='replacement directive uses new epoch' ;;
    11) registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='nonquiescent callback cannot capture' ;;
    12) registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='false path skips gate' ;;
    13) registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='unsuccessful exact frame times out phase' ;;
    14) registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='frame pre-overrun precedes path' ;;
    15) registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='frame pre-overrun is capped' ;;
    16) registration='nonquiescent breaks pair'; fragment='capture exception Error code' ;;
    17) registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='Initial flush precedes Ready' ;;
    18) registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='Ready failure follows durable Initial at index 9' ;;
    19) registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='repeated observer failure writes one Error' ;;
    20) registration='prepare activate separation'; fragment='disabled boundary is inert before observer access' ;;
    21) registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='Error write failure uses trace marker' ;;
    22) registration='cardinal clears candidate'; fragment='cardinal input cleared neutral eligibility' ;;
    23) registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='same raw save with unequal complete signature does not settle' ;;
    24) registration='matching pair writes initial'; fragment='authorization precedes gate inspection' ;;
    25) registration='exact deadline and pre overrun'; fragment='Initial trace failure retains close ownership after Dispose' ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

r02_key_count() {
  local ledger_path="$1"
  local key="$2"
  LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r02 $key: " \
    'index($0,p) == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$ledger_path"
}

r02_key_value() {
  local ledger_path="$1"
  local key="$2"
  LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r02 $key: " \
    'index($0,p) == 1 { print substr($0,length(p)+1) }' "$ledger_path"
}

require_r02_pair_value() {
  local key="$1"
  local expected_value="$2"
  require_exact_output 1 r02_key_count "$REPORT_PATH" "$key" || return 1
  require_exact_output 1 r02_key_count "$PROGRESS_PATH" "$key" || return 1
  require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
    r02_key_value "$REPORT_PATH" "$key" || return 1
  require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
    r02_key_value "$PROGRESS_PATH" "$key" || return 1
}

validate_r02_binding() {
  local evidence_role="$1"
  local mutation_id="$2"
  local evidence_output
  local evidence_status
  local marker
  local historical_value
  local output_sha
  local status_sha
  r02_mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
  evidence_output="$ARTIFACT_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r02-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-output.txt"
  evidence_status="$ARTIFACT_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r02-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-status.txt"
  marker="Task 4.2 correction r02 $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
  test -f "$evidence_output" || return 1
  test ! -L "$evidence_output" || return 1
  test -f "$evidence_status" || return 1
  test ! -L "$evidence_status" || return 1
  output_sha="$(file_sha "$evidence_output")" || return 1
  status_sha="$(file_sha "$evidence_status")" || return 1
  historical_value="output-path=$evidence_output|output-sha256=$output_sha|status-path=$evidence_status|status-sha256=$status_sha|marker=$marker|registration=$registration|fragment=$fragment|status=1|result=KILLED|restoration=IMPLEMENTER_BASELINE|focused-green=PASS"
  require_r02_pair_value \
    "$evidence_role M$mutation_id evidence" "$historical_value" || return 1
}

r03_key_count() {
  local ledger_path="$1"
  local key="$2"
  LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r03 $key: " \
    'index($0,p) == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$ledger_path"
}

r03_key_value() {
  local ledger_path="$1"
  local key="$2"
  LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk -v p="Task 4.2 correction r03 $key: " \
    'index($0,p) == 1 { print substr($0,length(p)+1) }' "$ledger_path"
}

require_r03_pair_value() {
  local key="$1"
  local expected_value="$2"
  require_exact_output 1 r03_key_count "$REPORT_PATH" "$key" || return 1
  require_exact_output 1 r03_key_count "$PROGRESS_PATH" "$key" || return 1
  require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
    r03_key_value "$REPORT_PATH" "$key" || return 1
  require_exact_output "$expected_value" \
    r03_key_value "$PROGRESS_PATH" "$key" || return 1
}

validate_r03_binding() {
  local evidence_role="$1"
  local mutation_id="$2"
  local evidence_output
  local evidence_status
  local marker
  local historical_value
  local output_sha
  local status_sha
  r02_mutation_meta "$mutation_id" || return 1
  evidence_output="$ARTIFACT_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r03-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-output.txt"
  evidence_status="$ARTIFACT_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r03-$evidence_role-M$mutation_id-status.txt"
  marker="Task 4.2 correction r03 $evidence_role M$mutation_id"
  test -f "$evidence_output" || return 1
  test ! -L "$evidence_output" || return 1
  test -f "$evidence_status" || return 1
  test ! -L "$evidence_status" || return 1
  output_sha="$(file_sha "$evidence_output")" || return 1
  status_sha="$(file_sha "$evidence_status")" || return 1
  historical_value="output-path=$evidence_output|output-sha256=$output_sha|status-path=$evidence_status|status-sha256=$status_sha|marker=$marker|registration=$registration|fragment=$fragment|status=1|result=KILLED|restoration=GIT_CLEAN|focused-green=PASS"
  require_r03_pair_value \
    "$evidence_role M$mutation_id evidence" "$historical_value" || return 1
}
```
