- [ ] **Step 1a: Independently authenticate the marked preseal core.**

  This read-only bootstrap gate proves that the working candidate contains one
  ordered, nonempty core and that the exact bytes later used by the disposable
  harness match their independent line/byte/SHA pins and Apple Bash syntax.

```bash
/usr/bin/env -i PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin' LC_ALL=C \
  /bin/bash --noprofile --norc <<'TASK42_CORE_VERIFY'
set -euo pipefail
set +o braceexpand
cd /Users/jlsor/Documents/Research/SSR/.worktrees/ssr-oracle-passive-trace
PLAN_PATH='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
CORE_LINES=1525
CORE_BYTES=56564
CORE_SHA='80c4b248a07dafcde6d5ca436f0dc3ba6086845f64c6df8e4155b3bd114e9afa'

extract_core() {
  /usr/bin/awk '
    BEGIN {
      begin_marker = "# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_BEGIN"
      end_marker = "# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_END"
    }
    $0 == begin_marker {
      begin_count++
      if (begin_count != 1 || end_count != 0 || inside) exit 91
      inside = 1
      next
    }
    $0 == end_marker {
      end_count++
      if (begin_count != 1 || end_count != 1 || !inside) exit 91
      inside = 0
      next
    }
    inside { body = body $0 ORS; body_lines++ }
    END {
      if (begin_count != 1 || end_count != 1 || inside || body_lines == 0)
        exit 91
      printf "%s", body
    }
  ' "$PLAN_PATH"
}

actual_lines="$(extract_core | /usr/bin/wc -l | /usr/bin/tr -d '[:space:]')" || exit 1
actual_bytes="$(extract_core | /usr/bin/wc -c | /usr/bin/tr -d '[:space:]')" || exit 1
actual_sha="$(extract_core | /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 |
  /usr/bin/awk 'NR == 1 { print $1 }')" || exit 1
/bin/test "$actual_lines" = "$CORE_LINES" || exit 1
/bin/test "$actual_bytes" = "$CORE_BYTES" || exit 1
/bin/test "$actual_sha" = "$CORE_SHA" || exit 1
extract_core | /bin/bash -n || exit 1
TASK42_CORE_VERIFY
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the private-preseal publication matrix only in a disposable
  `/private/tmp` tree.**

This probe never changes the repository and never names a canonical evidence
path. It dynamically extracts the exact marked preseal core from this plan,
requires its pinned line count, byte count, SHA-256, and syntax, and executes
that same core through the Task-8-equivalent mode/dispatch, producer-consumer
`pipefail`, `run_driver`, and launcher-capture path. Its default-inert seams are
accepted only by this nested synthetic `/private/tmp` namespace; the canonical
driver rejects every harness environment variable. The 15 cases inject exact
statuses 80, 81 with raw helper status 37, 82, 83, destination-side partial 84,
output-only 85,
postpublication and row-closure 86, precommit and committed-residual 126, and
signals 129/130/143, plus success under parent umasks 022 and 077. Each case
asserts canonical and private inventories, honest `setup-residual`,
`exact-bundle`, or `cleanup-residual` classification, descriptor effects,
ledger row counts, child `umask 022` mode 644, and public/private mode 600.
Stage 84 creates the exact known canonical destination partial while
noclobber is active, leaves the private three-file bundle intact, and requires
the real following publication redirect to fail rather than altering its
source.
Every deletion is an authenticated exact synthetic file or empty directory;
no recursive deletion is used. The harness substitutes only disposable suite,
ledger, manifest, and repository gates; it never invokes the real helper,
tests, build, canonical driver entry point, or any `r01`--`r04` artifact.

```bash
/usr/bin/env -i TMPDIR='/private/tmp' PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin' \
  LC_ALL=C /bin/bash --noprofile --norc <<'TASK42_PRIVATE_MATRIX'
set -euo pipefail
set +o braceexpand
umask 077
PLAN_PATH='/Users/jlsor/Documents/Research/SSR/.worktrees/ssr-oracle-passive-trace/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
CORE_LINES=1525
CORE_BYTES=56564
CORE_SHA='80c4b248a07dafcde6d5ca436f0dc3ba6086845f64c6df8e4155b3bd114e9afa'
ROOT="$(/usr/bin/mktemp -d \
  /private/tmp/task-4.2-r04-core-matrix.XXXXXX)" || exit 1
case "$ROOT" in
  /private/tmp/task-4.2-r04-core-matrix.??????) ;;
  *) exit 126 ;;
esac
test -d "$ROOT" || exit 1
test ! -L "$ROOT" || exit 1
test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$ROOT")" = 700 || exit 1
CORE_PATH="$ROOT/preseal-core.sh"

require_exact_output() {
  local actual_output
  local expected_output
  expected_output="$1"
  shift
  actual_output="$("$@")" || return 1
  test "$actual_output" = "$expected_output" || return 1
}

file_lines() {
  /usr/bin/awk 'END { print NR }' "$1"
}

file_bytes() {
  /usr/bin/wc -c < "$1" | /usr/bin/tr -d '[:space:]'
}

file_sha() {
  /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 "$1" | /usr/bin/awk '{print $1}'
}

extract_core() {
  /usr/bin/awk '
    BEGIN {
      begin_marker = "# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_BEGIN"
      end_marker = "# TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_CORE_END"
    }
    $0 == begin_marker {
      begin_count++
      if (begin_count != 1 || end_count != 0 || inside) exit 91
      inside = 1
      next
    }
    $0 == end_marker {
      end_count++
      if (begin_count != 1 || end_count != 1 || !inside) exit 91
      inside = 0
      next
    }
    inside { body = body $0 ORS; body_lines++ }
    END {
      if (begin_count != 1 || end_count != 1 || inside || body_lines == 0)
        exit 91
      printf "%s", body
    }
  ' "$PLAN_PATH"
}

extract_core > "$CORE_PATH" || exit 1
/bin/chmod 600 "$CORE_PATH" || exit 1
require_exact_output "$CORE_LINES" file_lines "$CORE_PATH" || exit 1
require_exact_output "$CORE_BYTES" file_bytes "$CORE_PATH" || exit 1
require_exact_output "$CORE_SHA" file_sha "$CORE_PATH" || exit 1
/bin/bash -n "$CORE_PATH" || exit 1

emit_harness() {
  /bin/cat "$CORE_PATH" || return 1
  /bin/cat <<'TASK42_CORE_HARNESS'
set -euo pipefail
set +o braceexpand
test "$#" = 7 || exit 64
mode="$1"
MATRIX_CASE="$2"
CASE_ROOT="$3"
MATRIX_ROOT="$4"
MATRIX_HELPER_STATUS="$5"
TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_POINT="$6"
PARENT_UMASK="$7"
test "$mode" = preseal || exit 64
case "$MATRIX_ROOT" in
  /private/tmp/task-4.2-r04-core-matrix.??????) ;;
  *) exit 126 ;;
esac
case "$CASE_ROOT" in "$MATRIX_ROOT"/case-*) ;; *) exit 126 ;; esac
case "$MATRIX_HELPER_STATUS" in 0|37) ;; *) exit 64 ;; esac
case "$PARENT_UMASK" in 022|077) ;; *) exit 64 ;; esac

TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_HARNESS='task-4.2-r04-synthetic-v1'
TASK42_R04_PRESEAL_PRIVATE_PREFIX="$MATRIX_ROOT/private-preseal"
artifact_dir="$CASE_ROOT/public"
report_path="$CASE_ROOT/report.md"
progress_path="$CASE_ROOT/progress.md"
suite_path="$CASE_ROOT/suite.sh"
row_prefix='Task 4.2 correction r04'
plan_anchor='0000000000000000000000000000000000000000'
helper_sha='synthetic-helper-sha256'
status_zero_sha='9a271f2a916b0b6ee6cecb2426f0b3206ef074578be55d9bc94f6f3fe3ab86aa'
suite_harness_line='SSR oracle unit harness ready'
suite_python_summary='1822 passed, 120 xfailed, 6 xpassed'
export CASE_ROOT MATRIX_HELPER_STATUS

file_lines() {
    /usr/bin/awk 'END { print NR }' "$1"
}

sha256_file() {
    /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 "$1" | /usr/bin/awk '{print $1}'
}

require_exact_output() {
    local actual_output
    local expected_output
    expected_output="$1"
    shift
    actual_output="$("$@")" || return 1
    test "$actual_output" = "$expected_output" || return 1
}

require_absent() {
    test ! -e "$1" || return 1
    test ! -L "$1" || return 1
}

key_count() {
    local ledger_key
    local ledger_path
    ledger_path="$1"
    ledger_key="$2"
    /usr/bin/awk -v sought="$row_prefix $ledger_key:" \
        'index($0,sought) == 1 { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$ledger_path"
}

require_pair_value() {
    local expected_line
    local ledger_key
    local ledger_value
    ledger_key="$1"
    ledger_value="$2"
    expected_line="$row_prefix $ledger_key: $ledger_value"
    require_exact_output 1 /usr/bin/awk -v line="$expected_line" \
        '$0 == line { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$report_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output 1 /usr/bin/awk -v line="$expected_line" \
        '$0 == line { n++ } END { print n+0 }' "$progress_path" || return 1
}

verify_ledgers() {
    local expected_rows
    expected_rows="$1"
    require_exact_output "$expected_rows" file_lines "$report_path" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_rows" file_lines "$progress_path" || return 1
    /usr/bin/cmp -s "$report_path" "$progress_path" || return 1
}

verify_manifest() {
    local canonical_output
    local canonical_status
    canonical_output="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt"
    canonical_status="$artifact_dir/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt"
    case "$1" in
        53)
            require_absent "$canonical_output" || return 1
            require_absent "$canonical_status" || return 1
            ;;
        54)
            require_owned_regular_mode "$canonical_output" 600 || return 1
            require_owned_regular_mode "$canonical_status" 600 || return 1
            require_not_alias "$canonical_output" "$canonical_status" || return 1
            ;;
        *) return 1 ;;
    esac
}

require_common() {
    test -d "$artifact_dir" || return 1
    test ! -L "$artifact_dir" || return 1
    require_owned_regular_mode "$report_path" 600 || return 1
    require_owned_regular_mode "$progress_path" 600 || return 1
    test -f "$suite_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$suite_path" || return 1
    test -x "$suite_path" || return 1
}

require_round_baseline() {
    require_common || return 1
}

require_helper_row() {
    test -f "$suite_path" || return 1
    test ! -L "$suite_path" || return 1
    test -x "$suite_path" || return 1
}

suite_value() {
    printf '%s' \
        'path=synthetic-suite.sh|sha256=synthetic|status=0|result=PASS'
}

task42_preseal_harness_hook() {
    local point
    point="$1"
    case "$MATRIX_CASE:$point" in
        setup80:setup-after-mkdir) return 77 ;;
        stage82:after-helper) return 77 ;;
        stage83:prepublication) return 77 ;;
        stage84:output-before-copy)
            /usr/bin/printf '%s\n' 'synthetic-partial-output' \
                > "$preseal_output" || return 77
            return 0
            ;;
        stage85:status-before-copy) return 77 ;;
        post86:postpublication-before-validation)
            printf '%s\n' 'synthetic error' >> "$preseal_output" || return 77
            return 0
            ;;
        closure86:closure-before-row111) return 77 ;;
        cleanup126:cleanup-precommit) return 77 ;;
        committed126:cleanup-after-first-unlink) return 77 ;;
        signal-hup:after-bundle)
            /bin/kill -HUP "$$"
            return 125
            ;;
        signal-int:prepublication)
            /bin/kill -INT "$$"
            return 125
            ;;
        signal-term:after-output)
            /bin/kill -TERM "$$"
            return 125
            ;;
    esac
    return 0
}

umask "$PARENT_UMASK"
task42_preseal_dispatch
TASK42_CORE_HARNESS
}

run_driver() {
  emit_harness |
    /usr/bin/env -i PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin' LC_ALL=C \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -n -s || return "$?"
  emit_harness |
    /usr/bin/env -i PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin' LC_ALL=C \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -s -- "$@"
}

locate_private() {
  local candidate
  local count
  count=0
  PRIVATE_DIR=''
  for candidate in "$ROOT"/private-preseal.??????; do
    if [ ! -e "$candidate" ] && [ ! -L "$candidate" ]; then
      continue
    fi
    count=$((count + 1))
    PRIVATE_DIR="$candidate"
  done
  test "$count" = 1
}

assert_no_private() {
  local candidate
  for candidate in "$ROOT"/private-preseal.??????; do
    if [ -e "$candidate" ] || [ -L "$candidate" ]; then
      return 1
    fi
  done
}

create_case() {
  local case_name
  local ledger_row
  case_name="$1"
  CASE_ROOT="$ROOT/case-$case_name"
  PUBLIC_DIR="$CASE_ROOT/public"
  REPORT_PATH="$CASE_ROOT/report.md"
  PROGRESS_PATH="$CASE_ROOT/progress.md"
  SUITE_PATH="$CASE_ROOT/suite.sh"
  LAUNCHER_OUT="$CASE_ROOT/launcher.out"
  LAUNCHER_ERR="$CASE_ROOT/launcher.err"
  /bin/mkdir -m 700 "$CASE_ROOT" || return 1
  /bin/mkdir -m 700 "$PUBLIC_DIR" || return 1
  ledger_row=1
  while [ "$ledger_row" -le 110 ]; do
    /usr/bin/printf 'synthetic-ledger-%03d\n' "$ledger_row" \
      >> "$REPORT_PATH" || return 1
    /usr/bin/printf 'synthetic-ledger-%03d\n' "$ledger_row" \
      >> "$PROGRESS_PATH" || return 1
    ledger_row=$((ledger_row + 1))
  done
  /bin/cat > "$SUITE_PATH" <<'TASK42_SYNTHETIC_SUITE'
#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
: > "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt"
printf '%s\n' \
  'Build succeeded.' \
  '    0 Warning(s)' \
  '    0 Error(s)' \
  'Build succeeded.' \
  '    0 Warning(s)' \
  '    0 Error(s)' \
  'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
  'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
  '1822 passed, 120 xfailed, 6 xpassed in 0.01s'
exit "$MATRIX_HELPER_STATUS"
TASK42_SYNTHETIC_SUITE
  /bin/chmod 600 "$REPORT_PATH" "$PROGRESS_PATH" || return 1
  /bin/chmod 700 "$SUITE_PATH" || return 1
}

assert_private_inventory() {
  local actual_inventory
  local entry
  local expected_inventory
  local name
  expected_inventory="$1"
  locate_private || return 1
  case "$PRIVATE_DIR" in
    "$ROOT"/private-preseal.??????) ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
  test -d "$PRIVATE_DIR" || return 1
  test ! -L "$PRIVATE_DIR" || return 1
  test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$PRIVATE_DIR")" = 700 || return 1
  actual_inventory=''
  for name in diagnostic output status; do
    entry="$PRIVATE_DIR/$name.txt"
    if [ -e "$entry" ] || [ -L "$entry" ]; then
      test -f "$entry" || return 1
      test ! -L "$entry" || return 1
      test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$entry")" = 600 || return 1
      if [ -n "$actual_inventory" ]; then
        actual_inventory="$actual_inventory,"
      fi
      actual_inventory="$actual_inventory$name"
    fi
  done
  for entry in "$PRIVATE_DIR"/* "$PRIVATE_DIR"/.[!.]* "$PRIVATE_DIR"/..?*; do
    if [ ! -e "$entry" ] && [ ! -L "$entry" ]; then continue; fi
    case "$entry" in
      "$PRIVATE_DIR/diagnostic.txt"|"$PRIVATE_DIR/output.txt"|\
      "$PRIVATE_DIR/status.txt") ;;
      *) return 1 ;;
    esac
  done
  test "$actual_inventory" = "$expected_inventory" || return 1
}

assert_public_inventory() {
  local actual_inventory
  local entry
  local expected_inventory
  local name
  expected_inventory="$1"
  actual_inventory=''
  for name in output status; do
    entry="$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-$name.txt"
    if [ -e "$entry" ] || [ -L "$entry" ]; then
      test -f "$entry" || return 1
      test ! -L "$entry" || return 1
      test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$entry")" = 600 || return 1
      if [ -n "$actual_inventory" ]; then
        actual_inventory="$actual_inventory,"
      fi
      actual_inventory="$actual_inventory$name"
    fi
  done
  for entry in "$PUBLIC_DIR"/* "$PUBLIC_DIR"/.[!.]* "$PUBLIC_DIR"/..?*; do
    if [ ! -e "$entry" ] && [ ! -L "$entry" ]; then continue; fi
    case "$entry" in
      "$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt"|\
      "$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt") ;;
      *) return 1 ;;
    esac
  done
  test "$actual_inventory" = "$expected_inventory" || return 1
}

  CASE_HELPER_STATUS=0

      EXPECTED_FAILURE_STAGE_CODE=83
      EXPECTED_DIAG_TRACE='preflight|helper|private-validation|prepublication'

      ;;
    stage84)

      EXPECTED_FAILURE_STAGE_CODE=85

    post86)

      EXPECTED_PUBLIC_OUTPUT=forbidden-diagnostic
      EXPECTED_STAGE=postpublication
      EXPECTED_PREDICATE=forbidden-diagnostic
      EXPECTED_FAILURE_STAGE_CODE=86

    closure86)

      EXPECTED_DIAGNOSTIC_LINES=17

    committed126)

    success022|success077)

      EXPECTED_PUBLIC='output,status'
      EXPECTED_ROWS=112
      EXPECTED_CLASS=none
      EXPECTED_BUNDLE=0
      EXPECTED_DIAGNOSTIC=cleaned
      EXPECTED_DIAGNOSTIC_LINES=0
      EXPECTED_EXIT_RECORD=0
      EXPECTED_STAGE=NONE
      EXPECTED_PREDICATE=NONE
      EXPECTED_DIAG_TRACE=''
      if [ "$expectation_case" = success022 ]; then
        CASE_PARENT_UMASK=022
      fi
      ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

emit_expected_diagnostic() {
  local expected_trace_stage
  local old_ifs
  printf '%s\n' \
    'schema=task-4.2-correction-r04-private-preseal-v1' \
    'plan-anchor=0000000000000000000000000000000000000000' \
    'helper-sha256=synthetic-helper-sha256' \
    'private-create-umask=077' \
    'suite-child-umask=022' \
    'private-bundle-complete=1' || return 1
  old_ifs="$IFS"
  IFS='|'
  for expected_trace_stage in $EXPECTED_DIAG_TRACE; do
    printf 'stage=%s\n' "$expected_trace_stage" || return 1
  done
  IFS="$old_ifs"
  if [ "$EXPECTED_CLEANUP" = 1 ]; then
    printf '%s\n' \
      'cleanup-commit=authorized|private-inventory=exact-three|destruction=output,status,diagnostic,directory' || return 1
  fi
  if [ "$EXPECTED_FAILURE_STAGE_CODE" != NONE ]; then
    printf '%s\n' \
      "failure-stage=$EXPECTED_STAGE|predicate=$EXPECTED_PREDICATE|stage-code=$EXPECTED_FAILURE_STAGE_CODE|helper-status=$EXPECTED_HELPER_DIAG" || return 1
  fi
  if [ "$EXPECTED_EXIT_RECORD" = 1 ]; then
    printf '%s\n' \
      "exit-stage=$EXPECTED_STAGE|predicate=$EXPECTED_PREDICATE|exit=$EXPECTED_STATUS|helper-status=$EXPECTED_HELPER_DIAG|private-state=$EXPECTED_CLASS|residual-auth=PASS|bundle-complete=$EXPECTED_BUNDLE|cleanup-committed=$EXPECTED_CLEANUP" || return 1
  fi
}

assert_exact_diagnostic() {
  local actual_diagnostic_base64
  local diagnostic_path
  local expected_diagnostic_base64
  diagnostic_path="$PRIVATE_DIR/diagnostic.txt"
  case "$EXPECTED_DIAGNOSTIC" in
    absent)
      test ! -e "$diagnostic_path" || return 1
      test ! -L "$diagnostic_path" || return 1
      ;;
    cleaned)
      assert_no_private || return 1
      ;;
    present)
      test -f "$diagnostic_path" || return 1
      test ! -L "$diagnostic_path" || return 1
      test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$diagnostic_path")" = 600 || return 1
      require_exact_output "$EXPECTED_DIAGNOSTIC_LINES" \
        file_lines "$diagnostic_path" || return 1
      expected_diagnostic_base64="$(emit_expected_diagnostic |
        /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
      actual_diagnostic_base64="$(/usr/bin/base64 < "$diagnostic_path")" || return 1
      test "$actual_diagnostic_base64" = \
        "$expected_diagnostic_base64" || return 1
      ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

assert_exact_stderr() {
  local expected_residual
  local noclobber_detail
  local noclobber_line
  local noclobber_line_number
  local noclobber_prefix
  local partial_output
  test -f "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
  test ! -L "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
  test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$LAUNCHER_ERR")" = 600 || return 1
  if [ "$EXPECTED_STATUS" = 0 ]; then
    require_exact_output 0 file_lines "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
    test ! -s "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
    return 0
  fi
  expected_residual="Task 4.2 correction r04 private preseal residual: path=$PRIVATE_DIR|private-state=$EXPECTED_CLASS|residual-auth=PASS|bundle-complete=$EXPECTED_BUNDLE|cleanup-committed=$EXPECTED_CLEANUP|stage=$EXPECTED_STAGE|predicate=$EXPECTED_PREDICATE|exit=$EXPECTED_STATUS|helper-status=$EXPECTED_HELPER_DIAG"
  if [ "$case_name" = stage84 ]; then
    partial_output="$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt"
    require_exact_output 2 file_lines "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
    noclobber_line="$(/usr/bin/sed -n '1p' "$LAUNCHER_ERR")" || return 1
    noclobber_prefix='/bin/bash: line '
    case "$noclobber_line" in "$noclobber_prefix"*) ;; *) return 1 ;; esac
    noclobber_detail="${noclobber_line#"$noclobber_prefix"}"
    noclobber_line_number="${noclobber_detail%%:*}"
    case "$noclobber_line_number" in
      ''|*[!0-9]*) return 1 ;;
    esac
    test "$noclobber_detail" = \
      "$noclobber_line_number: $partial_output: cannot overwrite existing file" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_residual" \
      /usr/bin/sed -n '2p' "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
  else
    require_exact_output 1 file_lines "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
    require_exact_output "$expected_residual" \
      /usr/bin/sed -n '1p' "$LAUNCHER_ERR" || return 1
  fi
}

cleanup_case() {
  local entry
  if locate_private; then
    for entry in "$PRIVATE_DIR/diagnostic.txt" "$PRIVATE_DIR/output.txt" \
      "$PRIVATE_DIR/status.txt"; do
      if [ -e "$entry" ] || [ -L "$entry" ]; then
        test -f "$entry" || return 1
        test ! -L "$entry" || return 1
        /bin/rm "$entry" || return 1
      fi
    done
    /bin/rmdir "$PRIVATE_DIR" || return 1
  fi
  for entry in \
    "$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-output.txt" \
    "$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-status.txt"; do
    if [ -e "$entry" ] || [ -L "$entry" ]; then
      test -f "$entry" || return 1
      test ! -L "$entry" || return 1
      /bin/rm "$entry" || return 1
    fi
  done
  /bin/rmdir "$PUBLIC_DIR" || return 1
  for entry in "$REPORT_PATH" "$PROGRESS_PATH" "$SUITE_PATH" \
    "$LAUNCHER_OUT" "$LAUNCHER_ERR" "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt"; do
    if [ -e "$entry" ] || [ -L "$entry" ]; then
      test -f "$entry" || return 1
      test ! -L "$entry" || return 1
      /bin/rm "$entry" || return 1
    fi
  done
  /bin/rmdir "$CASE_ROOT" || return 1
}

run_case() {
  local case_name
  local expected_base64
  local observed_status
  local observed_base64
  local private_name
  local private_path
  local public_name
  local public_path
  case_name="$1"
  test "$#" = 1 || return 1
  case_expectations "$case_name" || return 1

  assert_no_private || return 1
  create_case "$case_name" || return 1
  set +e
  run_driver preseal "$case_name" "$CASE_ROOT" "$ROOT" \
    "$CASE_HELPER_STATUS" "$CASE_POINT" "$CASE_PARENT_UMASK" \
    > "$LAUNCHER_OUT" 2> "$LAUNCHER_ERR"
  observed_status="$?"
  set -e
  test "$observed_status" = "$EXPECTED_STATUS" || return 1
  require_exact_output "$EXPECTED_ROWS" file_lines "$REPORT_PATH" || return 1
  require_exact_output "$EXPECTED_ROWS" file_lines "$PROGRESS_PATH" || return 1
  /usr/bin/cmp -s "$REPORT_PATH" "$PROGRESS_PATH" || return 1
  assert_public_inventory "$EXPECTED_PUBLIC" || return 1
  require_exact_output 0 file_lines "$LAUNCHER_OUT" || return 1
  test ! -s "$LAUNCHER_OUT" || return 1

  if [ "$EXPECTED_PRIVATE" = none ]; then
    assert_no_private || return 1
  else
    assert_private_inventory "$EXPECTED_PRIVATE" || return 1
  fi
  assert_exact_diagnostic || return 1
  assert_exact_stderr || return 1

  if [ "$EXPECTED_PRIVATE" != none ]; then
    for private_name in output status; do
      private_path="$PRIVATE_DIR/$private_name.txt"
      case ",$EXPECTED_PRIVATE," in
        *,"$private_name",*)
          if [ "$private_name" = output ]; then
            case "$EXPECTED_PRIVATE_OUTPUT" in
              empty) expected_base64='' ;;
              helper)
                expected_base64="$(/usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
                  'Task 4.2 correction r04 preseal' \
                  'Build succeeded.' \
                  '    0 Warning(s)' \
                  '    0 Error(s)' \
                  'Build succeeded.' \
                  '    0 Warning(s)' \
                  '    0 Error(s)' \
                  'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
                  'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
                  '1822 passed, 120 xfailed, 6 xpassed in 0.01s' |
                  /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
                ;;
              *) return 1 ;;
            esac
          else
            case "$EXPECTED_PRIVATE_STATUS" in
              empty) expected_base64='' ;;
              helper-status)
                expected_base64="$(/usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
                  "$CASE_HELPER_STATUS" | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
                ;;
              *) return 1 ;;
            esac
          fi
          observed_base64="$(/usr/bin/base64 < "$private_path")" || return 1
          test "$observed_base64" = "$expected_base64" || return 1
          ;;
      esac
    done
  fi

  for public_name in output status; do
    public_path="$PUBLIC_DIR/task-4.2-correction-r04-preseal-$public_name.txt"
    case ",$EXPECTED_PUBLIC," in
      *,"$public_name",*)
        if [ "$public_name" = output ]; then
          case "$EXPECTED_PUBLIC_OUTPUT" in
            partial)
              expected_base64="$(/usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
                'synthetic-partial-output' | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
              require_exact_output 1 file_lines "$public_path" || return 1
              require_exact_output 25 file_bytes "$public_path" || return 1
              ;;
            forbidden-diagnostic)
            expected_base64="$(/usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
              'Task 4.2 correction r04 preseal' \
              'Build succeeded.' \
              '    0 Warning(s)' \
              '    0 Error(s)' \
              'Build succeeded.' \
              '    0 Warning(s)' \
              '    0 Error(s)' \
              'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
              'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
              '1822 passed, 120 xfailed, 6 xpassed in 0.01s' \
              'synthetic error' | /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
              ;;
            helper)
              expected_base64="$(/usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
                'Task 4.2 correction r04 preseal' \
                'Build succeeded.' \
                '    0 Warning(s)' \
                '    0 Error(s)' \
                'Build succeeded.' \
                '    0 Warning(s)' \
                '    0 Error(s)' \
                'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
                'SSR oracle unit harness ready' \
                '1822 passed, 120 xfailed, 6 xpassed in 0.01s' |
                /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
              ;;
            *) return 1 ;;
          esac
        else
          expected_base64="$(/usr/bin/printf '%s\n' 0 |
            /usr/bin/base64)" || return 1
        fi
        observed_base64="$(/usr/bin/base64 < "$public_path")" || return 1
        test "$observed_base64" = "$expected_base64" || return 1
        ;;
    esac
  done

  if [ "$EXPECTED_HELPER_DIAG" != NOT-RUN ]; then
    test -f "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt" || return 1
    test ! -L "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt" || return 1
    test "$(stat -f '%Lp' "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt")" = 644 || return 1
  else
    test ! -e "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt" || return 1
    test ! -L "$CASE_ROOT/helper-default.txt" || return 1
  fi
  cleanup_case || return 1
  assert_no_private || return 1
}
```

## Implementation Tasks

### Task 1: Authenticate the plan anchor and begin both append-only suffixes

**Files:**

- Read: both tracked plans, both designs, correction brief, shared helper,
  production/tests, 25 patches, both ledgers
- Append only: `task-4.2-report.md`, `progress.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: Require exact anchor topology and the 26/108 manifest state.**

  Run the invocation pattern above with mode `anchor` and `P=HEAD`. It must
  begin from exact pristine ledger prefixes and append rows 1--44 only after
  recomputing every Git/file value. Expected final suffix length: 44 rows.

- [ ] **Step 2: Re-run the anchor mode as an idempotent verifier.**

  A second invocation must append nothing, validate all 44 values against the
  repository and artifacts, and leave tracked Git state clean. Execute both
  serialized invocations with this complete task-local launcher:

```bash
/usr/bin/env -i \
  TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
  PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
  LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
  /bin/bash --noprofile --norc <<'TASK42_LAUNCHER'
set -euo pipefail
PLAN_PATH='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
DRIVER_SHA='247f7a9f6c9979a0edbc8b5236f99ab584d2775ce6c437d443c72423ebcd1667'
P="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git rev-parse HEAD)" || exit 1
/bin/test "${#P}" = 40 || exit 1
case "$P" in *[!0-9a-f]*) exit 64 ;; esac
verified_p="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git \
  rev-parse --verify "$P^{commit}")" || exit 1
/bin/test "$verified_p" = "$P" || exit 1

extract_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git --no-pager show "$p:$PLAN_PATH" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk \
      -v begin='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
      -v end='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_END' '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
  bc++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {
  ec++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
  if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}

driver_body_sha() {
  extract_driver "$1" |
    /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk '
{
  n++
  if (n != 1 || NF != 2 || $2 != "-" || length($1) != 64 ||
      $1 ~ /[^0-9a-f]/) bad=1
  value=$1
}
END {
  if (bad || n != 1) exit 74
  print value
}'
}

emit_runnable_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  local rc
  if extract_driver "$p"; then
    /usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
      '[ "${TASK42_DRIVER_READY-}" = task-4.2-correction-r04-driver-complete-v1 ] || exit 125' \
      'task42_main_type="$(builtin type -t task42_main)" || exit 125' \
      '[ "$task42_main_type" = function ] || exit 125' \
      'task42_main "$@" </dev/null'
  else
    rc=$?
    return "$rc"
  fi
}

run_driver() {
  local actual_driver_sha
  local driver_status
  local p
  /bin/test "$#" -ge 2 || return 1
  p="$2"
  actual_driver_sha="$(driver_body_sha "$p")" || return 1
  /bin/test "$actual_driver_sha" = "$DRIVER_SHA" || return 1
  emit_runnable_driver "$p" |
    /usr/bin/env -i \
      TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
      PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
      LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -n -s -- "$@" || return 1
  actual_driver_sha="$(driver_body_sha "$p")" || return 1
  /bin/test "$actual_driver_sha" = "$DRIVER_SHA" || return 1
  set +e
  emit_runnable_driver "$p" |
    /usr/bin/env -i \
      TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
      PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
      LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -s -- "$@"
  driver_status="$?"
  set -e
  return "$driver_status"
}

run_driver anchor "$P" || exit 1
run_driver anchor "$P" || exit 1
TASK42_LAUNCHER
```

### Task 2: Authenticate the inherited corrected-test baseline

**Files:** read-only; no tracked or ignored path may change

- [ ] **Step 1: Verify the exact clean gate-only baseline.**

  Invoke the embedded driver with mode `baseline`, `P=HEAD`, and no other
  arguments. It must require 44 canonical ledger rows, active manifest 26/108,
  rejected manifests `r01=26/108`, `r02=82/52`, and `r03=132/2`, exact inherited corrected
  test hashes/counts, unchanged production/registry hashes, empty index, no
  tracked worktree diff, no non-ignored untracked path, and clean diff syntax.

```bash
/usr/bin/env -i \
  TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
  PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
  LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
  /bin/bash --noprofile --norc <<'TASK42_LAUNCHER'
set -euo pipefail
PLAN_PATH='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
DRIVER_SHA='247f7a9f6c9979a0edbc8b5236f99ab584d2775ce6c437d443c72423ebcd1667'
P="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git rev-parse HEAD)" || exit 1
/bin/test "${#P}" = 40 || exit 1
case "$P" in *[!0-9a-f]*) exit 64 ;; esac
verified_p="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git \
  rev-parse --verify "$P^{commit}")" || exit 1
/bin/test "$verified_p" = "$P" || exit 1

extract_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git --no-pager show "$p:$PLAN_PATH" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk \

  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {

  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {

  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}
```

### Task 6: Obtain four fresh independent approvals

**Files:** read-only; append only both ledgers after each accepted return

Dispatch four fresh subagents from exact clean P4, one per review below. Each
prompt must name `r04`, exact P4, inherited T2 and P2, the approved design,
this plan at P4, the correction brief/hash, and its review remit. Every prompt
must prohibit
direct execution of the ignored forced-suite helper and any build, mutation,
evidence, ledger, index, or HEAD write in the shared worktree. A reviewer that
needs a live compatibility probe must use a separate disposable copy, remain
offline, and may not import or write accepted-round evidence. Require a final
line containing the literal 40-hex P4 after `TIP=`, followed by
`VERDICT=APPROVE CRITICAL=0 IMPORTANT=0`. The controller must read the entire
response—not merely its final line—and reject `r04` if any Critical or
Important finding appears anywhere. Review workers are read-only; no mutation,
evidence, ledger, index, or HEAD operation may overlap them.

- [ ] **Step 1: Specification/Task-5 transfer review.**

  Use exact identity `/root/task_4_2_r04_specification_review`. Review the full
  descendant chain, approved specification, exact gate-only scope, retained
  M14/M15/M24 classification, and Task 5.1--5.3 ownership/qualification.

- [ ] **Step 2: Behavioral-test/mutation review.**

  Use exact identity `/root/task_4_2_r04_behavioral_mutation_review`. Review
  supported-flow M03, independent M07/M10, all 25 patch bodies/hashes/oracles,
  test order, and fresh evidence semantics.

- [ ] **Step 3: Shell/evidence-gate review.**

  Use exact identity `/root/task_4_2_r04_shell_gate_review`. Review Apple Bash
  3.2 parsing/portability, commit-pinned extraction, no-clobber capture,
  authenticated inverse, manifest, 112-row suffix, seal, and terminal gate.

- [ ] **Step 4: Compatibility/platform review.**

  Use exact identity `/root/task_4_2_r04_compatibility_review`. Review C# 7.3,
  CLR-v2/net35, .NET 10, and macOS Tahoe 26.6 compatibility and rerun safe
  exact-tip verification as needed.

  ```text
  shell-gate /root/task_4_2_r04_shell_gate_review {exact-P4} APPROVE 0 0
  compatibility /root/task_4_2_r04_compatibility_review {exact-P4} APPROVE 0 0
  ```

- [ ] **Step 1: Invoke mode `controller` with `P=HEAD`.**

  Start Git-clean from exact `P4=HEAD`. Revalidate four reviews and all prior
  artifacts, replay M01--M25 independently, require Git-clean restoration and
  fresh focused GREEN after every inverse, and append rows 85--109 plus the
  controller summary row 110. Expected state: 110 ledger rows and manifest
  132/2.

```bash

  TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
  PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
  LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \

set -euo pipefail
PLAN_PATH='docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-04-task-4.2-review-correction.md'
DRIVER_SHA='247f7a9f6c9979a0edbc8b5236f99ab584d2775ce6c437d443c72423ebcd1667'
P="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git rev-parse HEAD)" || exit 1
/bin/test "${#P}" = 40 || exit 1
case "$P" in *[!0-9a-f]*) exit 64 ;; esac
verified_p="$(GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git \
  rev-parse --verify "$P^{commit}")" || exit 1
/bin/test "$verified_p" = "$P" || exit 1

extract_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git --no-pager show "$p:$PLAN_PATH" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk \
      -v begin='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
      -v end='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_END' '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
  bc++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {
  ec++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
  if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}

{

  value=$1
}

  print value
}'
}

  else

}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Invoke mode `preseal` with `P=HEAD`.**

  their exact inventory, modes, ownership, non-aliasing, and fixed descriptors
  all pass. Write and verify exact private status `0` with the pinned status SHA
  while canonical manifest state remains 53. After one prepublication recheck,

  public files byte-for-byte to the private pair; and require manifest 54.

```bash
extract_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 /usr/bin/git --no-pager show "$p:$PLAN_PATH" |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk \
      -v begin='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_BEGIN' \
      -v end='# TASK42_CORRECTION_R04_DRIVER_END' '
BEGIN { bc=0; ec=0; inside=0; bad=0; n=0 }
$0 == begin {
  bc++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 0 || inside != 0) bad=1
  inside=1
  next
}
$0 == end {
  ec++
  if (bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 1) bad=1
  inside=0
  next
}
inside { body[++n]=$0 }
END {
  if (bad || bc != 1 || ec != 1 || inside != 0 || n == 0) exit 73
  for (i=1; i<=n; i++) print body[i]
}'
}

driver_body_sha() {
  extract_driver "$1" |
    /usr/bin/shasum -a 256 |
    LC_ALL=C /usr/bin/awk '
{
  n++
  if (n != 1 || NF != 2 || $2 != "-" || length($1) != 64 ||
      $1 ~ /[^0-9a-f]/) bad=1
  value=$1
}
END {
  if (bad || n != 1) exit 74
  print value
}'
}

emit_runnable_driver() {
  local p="$1"
  local rc
  if extract_driver "$p"; then
    /usr/bin/printf '%s\n' \
      '[ "${TASK42_DRIVER_READY-}" = task-4.2-correction-r04-driver-complete-v1 ] || exit 125' \
      'task42_main_type="$(builtin type -t task42_main)" || exit 125' \
      '[ "$task42_main_type" = function ] || exit 125' \
      'task42_main "$@" </dev/null'
  else
    rc=$?
    return "$rc"
  fi
}

run_driver() {
  local actual_driver_sha
  local driver_status
  local p
  /bin/test "$#" -ge 2 || return 1
  p="$2"
  actual_driver_sha="$(driver_body_sha "$p")" || return 1
  /bin/test "$actual_driver_sha" = "$DRIVER_SHA" || return 1
  emit_runnable_driver "$p" |
    /usr/bin/env -i \
      TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
      PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
      LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -n -s -- "$@" || return 1
  actual_driver_sha="$(driver_body_sha "$p")" || return 1
  /bin/test "$actual_driver_sha" = "$DRIVER_SHA" || return 1
  set +e
  emit_runnable_driver "$p" |
    /usr/bin/env -i \
      TMPDIR='/private/tmp' \
      PATH='/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin:/opt/homebrew/bin:/opt/homebrew/Cellar/dotnet/10.0.300/bin' \
      LC_ALL=C GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null \
      /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -s -- "$@"
  driver_status="$?"
  set -e
  return "$driver_status"
}

run_driver pre-seal-commit "$P" || exit 1
TASK42_LAUNCHER
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Record the live terminal result only in the handoff.**

  Do not append the verifier result to a frozen ledger and do not create a
  recursive second seal. Task 4.2 correction is complete only from a freshly
  observed terminal exit status `0` at exact `S`.

## Failure and later-round protocol

Any test, mutation, review, gate, artifact, ledger, seal, or terminal failure
rejects `r04`; it does not authorize history repair. Preserve every current
commit, every ignored artifact, and every prior ledger byte—including a
malformed, partial, or one-ledger-only suffix. Pin the complete physical bytes
of each ledger as the next round's immutable prefix; do not normalize, finish,
truncate, or copy any rejected row into the new suffix.

Every `r04` failure starts `r05`; no `r04` launcher or artifact path may be
retried, repaired, completed, or reused. If no S4 seal commit exists, P5 must
be the exact two-plan-path child of rejected P4. If S4 exists and a later seal
validation or terminal gate fails, P5 must instead be the exact two-plan-path
child of rejected S4. There is no ancestry choice: first prove whether the
one-file S4 commit exists at the tracked seal path, then select that required
parent.

If any tracked evidence seal already exists, its frozen ledger contract is a
prerequisite to every later round. Parse each frozen path/byte-count/SHA row
strictly and uniquely from the exact seal commit; require each current physical
ledger to be at least that many bytes; and hash exactly its first sealed byte
count, requiring equality with the sealed SHA-256. Only after proving that the
entire previously sealed ledger is an unchanged prefix may the controller pin
the complete current physical bytes—including any later malformed or partial
suffix—as the next immutable prefix. If either sealed-prefix proof is
impossible, stop: no cleanup, new anchor, append, or replacement seal is
authorized.

Before creating the next anchor, restore only an uncommitted transient from the
rejected attempt: authenticate the exact forward patch already applied, use its
exact inverse, prove the production hashes and tracked scope, and remove an
uncommitted seal candidate from the index/worktree by the exact inverse of its
creation while preserving any committed seal and all ignored evidence. If that
exact restoration cannot be proven, stop; do not create the next round.

Before writing P5, authenticate S4 whenever it exists: require its exact P4
parent, seal subject, one-file scope, and strictly parsed frozen report/progress
prefixes, then authenticate those sealed prefixes against the current physical
ledgers before pinning any longer rejected bytes. Write the amended plan in the
new exact two-plan-path `r05` anchor with the conditional parent above. Give it
a new brief, artifact namespace, and complete prefix pins. Its S5 seal commit
must create or replace the same tracked seal path used by S4; never create a
competing seal path. Rerun all three mutation REDs, every GREEN gate, all 25
implementer transactions, four fresh reviews, all 25 controller replays,
pre-seal capture, seal validation, and terminal verification even when the
correction is gate-only. A gate-only later round has no test commit; a
test-changing later round may have one exact two-test-path commit. Never combine
evidence across rounds.

## Completion handoff

The final report names P4, rejected parent P3, inherited T2/P2, and S4; the tracked seal path; the
four review identities; the frozen ledger hashes; the post-seal verifier's observed zero
exit; exact net10/net35/focused/full/Python results; and confirmation that
production, Program, Task 5 behavior, game files, saves, installed
configuration, and historical evidence were unchanged.

S4 is the first canonical Task 4.2 evidence seal, but Task 4.2 is accepted only
after the live terminal verifier returns exact status `0` from S4. Task 5 is
explicitly blocked after exact S4 until a descendant Task 5 ownership/rebase
plan is written and approved. Do not execute any legacy Task 5.1--5.3 text.
The descendant plan must start from exact `S`, recompute every
test/document/helper hash and every affected patch context, remove or rewrite
the obsolete
`SetNeutralSeenForDefensiveTest(false)` call, and preserve the approved Task
5.1--5.3 ownership semantics. Only that descendant plan may qualify Task 5 for
execution.
